# CET-4 核心词汇精词汇表
## 7天 × 30词 | 上海高考真题语境版

---

## 温馨提示

> **保持专注，来回阅读/朗读该词汇表的每日单词即可。**
>
> **不要求默写/抄写/背诵。** 只需放松、自然地反复浏览和朗读，让这些词汇在脑海中留下印象和熟悉感即可。
>
> 目标：**熟悉，见面不陌生即可。**着重记忆**一词多义**的单词，是上海英语高考的重点
>

---

## Day 1

### 1. academic
**音标**：/ˌækəˈdemɪk/
**词性**：adj./n.
**中文释义**：学术的；大学生，学究
**英文释义**：relating to education and scholarship; a student or teacher at a college/university
**词根**：Greek *akademeia* (Plato's school) — from the garden of Akademos
**例句**：The **academic** performance of students improved significantly after the new teaching method was introduced.

---

### 2. access
**音标**：/ˈækses/
**词性**：n./v.
**中文释义**：进入权，使用权；访问，进入
**英文释义**：the means or opportunity to approach or enter; to reach or obtain
**词根**：Latin *accedere* (to approach) = *ad-* (to) + *cedere* (to go)
**例句**：Students need **access** to图书馆 resources to complete their research assignments.

---

### 3. achieve
**音标**：/əˈtʃiːv/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：达到，实现，获得
**英文释义**：to successfully reach a goal through effort; to accomplish
**词根**：Latin *ad* (to) + *caput* (head) — originally "to bring to a head"
**例句**：Through hard work and determination, she was able to **achieve** her dream of studying abroad.

---

### 4. acquire
**音标**：/əˈkwaɪə/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：获得，学到，收购
**英文释义**：to gain possession of something; to learn or develop a skill
**词根**：Latin *acquirere* = *ad-* (to) + *quirere* (to seek)
**例句**：The company plans to **acquire** its competitor to expand market share.

---

### 5. adequate
**音标**：/ˈædɪkwət/
**词性**：adj.
**中文释义**：足够的，适当的，勉强够的
**英文释义**：sufficient; enough for a particular purpose; satisfactory
**词根**：Latin *adaequare* (to make equal) = *ad-* (to) + *aequus* (equal)
**例句**：The hotel provided **adequate** facilities for a comfortable stay.

---

### 6. adjust
**音标**：/əˈdʒʌst/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：调整，调节，适应
**英文释义**：to alter slightly to achieve a desired result; to adapt
**词根**：Latin *ad-* (to) + *justus* (just, proper) — to put in proper order
**例句**：It took her a few weeks to **adjust** to the new schedule after moving to a different time zone.

---

### 7. administration
**音标**：/ədˌmɪnɪˈstreɪʃən/
**词性**：n.
**中文释义**：管理，行政，管理部门
**英文释义**：the process of managing or directing; the people who manage an organization
**词根**：Latin *administratio* = *ad-* (to) + *ministrare* (to serve, manage)
**例句**：The **administration** of the university announced new policies regarding campus safety.

---

### 8. affect
**音标**：/əˈfekt/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：影响，感动，作用于
**英文释义**：to produce a change in something; to influence or concern
**词根**：Latin *afficere* = *ad-* (to) + *facere* (to do, make)
**例句**：Climate change will **affect** agricultural production worldwide in the coming decades.

---

### 9. alternative
**音标**：/ɔːlˈtɜːnətɪv/
**词性**：adj./n.
**中文释义**：替代的，可选的；替代选择
**英文释义**：available as another possibility; something available as another choice
**词根**：Latin *alternare* (to alternate) from *alter* (other)
**例句**：The school offers **alternative** energy sources like solar panels to reduce electricity costs.

---

### 10. analyze
**音标**：/ˈænəlaɪz/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：分析，分解，解析
**英文释义**：to examine something in detail to understand it better
**词根**：Greek *analysis* = *ana-* (through) + *lyein* (to loosen) — breaking down into parts
**例句**：Scientists will **analyze** the data collected from the experiment to draw conclusions.

---

### 11. annual
**音标**：/ˈænjuəl/
**词性**：adj./n.
**中文释义**：每年的，年度的；年刊，年鉴
**英文释义**：occurring once a year; a publication issued once a year
**词根**：Latin *annus* (year) + *-al* (relating to)
**例句**：The university holds an **annual** ceremony to award scholarships to outstanding students.

---

### 12. apparent
**音标**：/əˈpærənt/
**词性**：adj.
**中文释义**：明显的，表面上的，显而易见的
**英文释义**：clearly visible or understood; seeming to be real but may not be
**词根**：Latin *apparere* (to appear) = *ad-* (to) + *parere* (to come forth)
**例句**：It was **apparent** from her expression that she had received good news.

---

### 13. approach
**音标**：/əˈprəʊtʃ/
**词性**：n./v.
**中文释义**：方法，途径；接近，靠近
**英文释义**：a way of dealing with something; to come near or nearer to
**词根**：Latin *appropiare* = *ad-* (to) + *prope* (near)
**例句**：The professor suggested a different **approach** to solving the complex mathematical problem.

---

### 14. appropriate
**音标**：/əˈprəʊpriət/
**词性**：adj./v.
**中文释义**：适当的，合适的；拨出（款项）
**英文释义**：suitable for a particular situation; to set aside for a specific purpose
**词根**：Latin *appropriare* = *ad-* (to) + *proprius* (one's own)
**例句**：It is **appropriate** to dress formally when attending a job interview.

---

### 15. area
**音标**：/ˈeəriə/
**词性**：n.
**中文释义**：地区，面积，领域
**英文释义**：a region or part of a place; a particular subject or activity
**词根**：Latin *area* (open space, courtyard)
**例句**：The coastal **area** experienced heavy rainfall last night.

---

### 16. aspect
**音标**：/ˈæspekt/
**词性**：n.
**中文释义**：方面，特征，外观
**英文释义**：a particular part or feature of something; the way something appears
**词根**：Latin *aspectus* = *ad-* (to) + *specere* (to look)
**例句**：We should consider every **aspect** of the plan before making a decision.

---

### 17. assess
**音标**：/əˈses/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：评估，评定，估算
**英文释义**：to evaluate or estimate the quality, importance, or value of something
**词根**：Latin *assidere* (to sit beside, hence to assist a judge) = *ad-* (to) + *sedere* (to sit)
**例句**：Teachers regularly **assess** students' progress through quizzes and assignments.

---

### 18. assign
**音标**：/əˈsaɪn/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：分配，指派，选派
**英文释义**：to allocate a task or duty to someone; to designate
**词根**：Latin *assignare* = *ad-* (to) + *signare* (to mark) — to mark out
**例句**：The teacher will **assign** homework at the end of each class session.

---

### 19. assist
**音标**：/əˈsɪst/
**词性**：v./n.
**中文释义**：帮助，援助，协助
**英文释义**：to help someone; assistance
**词根**：Latin *assistere* = *ad-* (to) + *sistere* (to stand) — to stand by
**例句**：The librarian can **assist** you in finding the books you need for your research.

---

### 20. assume
**音标**：/əˈsjuːm/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：假设，臆断，承担
**英文释义**：to suppose to be true without proof; to take on a responsibility
**词根**：Latin *assumere* = *ad-* (to) + *sumere* (to take)
**例句**：We should not **assume** that everyone shares the same opinion on this matter.

---

### 21. attend
**音标**：/əˈtend/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：出席，参加，照料
**英文释义**：to be present at an event; to give attention
**词根**：Latin *attendere* = *ad-* (to) + *tendere* (to stretch)
**例句**：All students are required to **attend** the orientation session next week.

---

### 22. available
**音标**：/əˈveɪləbəl/
**词性**：adj.
**中文释义**：可用的，可获得的，有空的
**英文释义**：able to be used or obtained; free to see or talk to people
**词根**：Latin *valere* (to be well, be worth) + *ad-* (to) — to be of value
**例句**：The concert tickets will be **available** for purchase starting next Monday.

---

### 23. basic
**音标**：/ˈbeɪsɪk/
**词性**：adj.
**中文释义**：基本的，基础的，根本的
**英文释义**：forming an essential foundation; fundamental
**词根**：Greek *basis* (foundation) + *-ic* (relating to)
**例句**：Understanding **basic** grammar is essential for effective communication in any language.

---

### 24. benefit
**音标**：/ˈbenɪfɪt/
**词性**：n./v.
**中文释义**：好处，利益；受益于，有助于
**英文释义**：an advantage or profit gained from something; to be helped by
**词根**：Latin *bene* (well) + *facere* (to do) — to do well
**例句**：Regular exercise has many **benefit** for both physical and mental health.

---

### 25. category
**音标**：/ˈkætəɡəri/
**词性**：n.
**中文释义**：类别，分类，类型
**英文释义**：a class or division of things having shared characteristics
**词根**：Greek *kategoria* (accusation, predicate) from *kategoros* (asserting)
**例句**：The books in the library are organized by **category** for easy access.

---

### 26. challenge
**音标**：/ˈtʃælɪndʒ/
**词性**：n./v.
**中文释义**：挑战，质疑；向…挑战
**英文释义**：a task or situation that tests someone's abilities; to question something
**词根**：Latin *calumnia* (false accusation) — originally a formal demand
**例句**：Learning a new language presents a **challenge** but also brings great rewards.

---

### 27. chapter
**音标**：/ˈtʃæptə/
**词性**：n.
**中文释义**：章节，篇章，重要时期
**英文释义**：a main division of a book; a period of time in history
**词根**：Latin *capitulum* (little head) from *caput* (head)
**例句**：The first **chapter** of the novel introduces the main characters and setting.

---

### 28. characteristic
**音标**：/ˌkærəktəˈrɪstɪk/
**词性**：n./adj.
**中文释义**：特征，特性；特有的，典型的
**英文释义**：a distinguishing feature or quality; typical of a person or thing
**词根**：Greek *charakter* (distinctive mark) + *-istic* (relating to)
**例句**：One of the **characteristic** features of this species is its bright blue feathers.

---

### 29. cite
**音标**：/saɪt/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：引用，引证，举出
**英文释义**：to mention something as evidence or support; to refer to
**词根**：Latin *citare* (to summon, rouse) from *ciere* (to move, summon)
**例句**：Remember to **cite** your sources properly when writing a research paper.

---

### 30. claim
**音标**：/kleɪm/
**词性**：v./n.
**中文释义**：声称，主张，要求；索赔
**英文释义**：to assert that something is true; to demand recognition or payment
**词根**：Latin *clamare* (to cry out)
**例句**：The scientist **claimed** that the new drug could effectively treat the disease.

---

## Day 2

### 31. classic
**音标**：/ˈklæsɪk/
**词性**：adj./n.
**中文释义**：经典的，典型的；经典作品
**英文释义**：of the highest quality; a work of enduring excellence
**词根**：Latin *classicus* (belonging to a class) from *classis* (a class)
**例句**："To Kill a Mockingbird" is considered a **classic** of American literature.

---

### 32. combine
**音标**：/kəmˈbaɪn/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：结合，合并，混合
**英文释义**：to join together to form a single unit; to mix together
**词根**：Latin *com-* (together) + *binus* (two by two) — to double together
**例句**：The recipe **combines** sweet and savory flavors in a unique way.

---

### 33. comment
**音标**：/ˈkɒment/
**词性**：n./v.
**中文释义**：评论，注释，意见
**英文释义**：a remark or observation expressing an opinion or judgment
**词根**：Latin *commentari* (to think over, note down) = *com-* (together) + *mens* (mind)
**例句**：The professor asked students to write **comments** on each other's essays.

---

### 34. communicate
**音标**：/kəˈmjuːnɪkeɪt/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：交流，沟通，传达
**英文释义**：to share or exchange information, ideas, or feelings
**词根**：Latin *communicare* (to share) from *communis* (common)
**例句**：It is important to **communicate** your thoughts clearly during a presentation.

---

### 35. community
**音标**：/kəˈmjuːnɪti/
**词性**：n.
**中文释义**：社区，团体，群落
**英文释义**：a group of people living in the same place; a group with shared interests
**词根**：Latin *communis* (common) + *-ity* (state of)
**例句**：The local **community** came together to clean up the park last weekend.

---

### 36. compare
**音标**：/kəmˈpeə/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：比较，相比，对照
**英文释义**：to examine similarities and differences between two or more things
**词根**：Latin *comparare* = *com-* (together) + *par* (equal)
**例句**：You should **compare** different products before making a purchasing decision.

---

### 37. compete
**音标**：/kəmˈpiːt/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：竞争，对抗，比赛
**英文释义**：to strive to gain something by defeating others
**词根**：Latin *competere* = *com-* (together) + *petere* (to seek)
**例句**：Students will **compete** for the scholarship by submitting their academic records.

---

### 38. complex
**音标**：/ˈkɒmpleks/
**词性**：adj./n.
**中文释义**：复杂的，复合的；综合体
**英文释义**：consisting of many interconnected parts; complicated
**词根**：Latin *complexus* = *com-* (together) + *plectere* (to weave)
**例句**：The **complex** relationship between supply and demand affects market prices.

---

### 39. concept
**音标**：/ˈkɒnsept/
**词性**：n.
**中文释义**：概念，观念，思想
**英文释义**：an abstract idea or general notion; a principle
**词根**：Latin *concipere* (to take in) = *con-* (together) + *capere* (to take)
**例句**：The **concept** of democracy varies across different cultures and historical periods.

---

### 40. concern
**音标**：/kənˈsɜːn/
**词性**：n./v.
**中文释义**：关心，担忧，关心的事
**英文释义**：worry or anxiety; to be relevant to; to involve
**词根**：Latin *concernere* = *con-* (together) + *cernere* (to separate, sift)
**例句**：Parents often express **concern** about their children's academic performance.

---

### 41. conclude
**音标**：/kənˈkluːd/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：结束，得出结论，断定
**英文释义**：to bring to an end; to reach a logical conclusion
**词根**：Latin *concludere* = *con-* (together) + *claudere* (to close)
**例句**：Based on the evidence, the jury was able to **conclude** that the defendant was guilty.

---

### 42. conduct
**音标**：/kənˈdʌkt/
**词性**：v./n.
**中文释义**：进行，实施，引导；行为举止
**英文释义**：to carry out an activity; the way a person acts
**词根**：Latin *conducere* = *con-* (together) + *ducere* (to lead)
**例句**：The experiment was conducted under strict laboratory conditions.

---

### 43. confirm
**音标**：/kənˈfɜːm/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：确认，证实，进一步确定
**英文释义**：to establish the truth or correctness of something
**词根**：Latin *confermare* = *con-* (intensive) + *firmare* (to make firm)
**例句**：Please **confirm** your attendance at the meeting by sending an email reply.

---

### 44. conflict
**音标**：/ˈkɒnflɪkt/
**词性**：n./v.
**中文释义**：冲突，矛盾，抵触
**英文释义**：a serious disagreement; to be incompatible
**词根**：Latin *confligere* = *con-* (together) + *fligere* (to strike)
**例句**：There was a **conflict** between the two departments over resource allocation.

---

### 45. consequence
**音标**：/ˈkɒnsɪkwəns/
**词性**：n.
**中文释义**：结果，后果，影响
**英文释义**：a result or effect of an action or condition
**词根**：Latin *consequi* = *con-* (together) + *sequi* (to follow)
**例句**：Every decision we make has **consequence** that affect our future.

---

### 46. consider
**音标**：/kənˈsɪdə/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：考虑，认为，体谅
**英文释义**：to think carefully about something; to take into account
**词根**：Latin *considerare* (to observe, consider) = *con-* (together) + *sidus* (star)
**例句**：You should **consider** all options before making a final decision.

---

### 47. constant
**音标**：/ˈkɒnstənt/
**词性**：adj./n.
**中文释义**：恒定的，不变的；常数
**英文释义**：occurring continuously; a value that does not change
**词根**：Latin *constans* = *con-* (standing) + *stare* (to stand) — standing firm
**例句**：The temperature remained **constant** throughout the experiment.

---

### 48. construct
**音标**：/kənˈstrʌkt/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：建造，构造，构筑
**英文释义**：to build or create something; to form by combining parts
**词根**：Latin *construere* = *con-* (together) + *struere* (to build)
**例句**：The new bridge was **constructed** to connect the two sides of the river.

---

### 49. consult
**音标**：/kənˈsʌlt/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：咨询，商量，查阅
**英文释义**：to seek advice or information from someone; to refer to
**词根**：Latin *consultare* (to deliberate) from *consuli* (to ask advice)
**例句**：You should **consult** a doctor if the symptoms persist for more than a week.

---

### 50. consume
**音标**：/kənˈsjuːm/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：消耗，消费，吃完，毁灭
**英文释义**：to use up a resource; to eat or drink; to destroy
**词根**：Latin *consumere* = *con-* (completely) + *sumere* (to take)
**例句**：Modern society **consumes** huge amounts of energy every day.

---

### 51. contain
**音标**：/kənˈteɪn/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：包含，容纳，遏制
**英文释义**：to hold or include within itself; to restrain
**词根**：Latin *continere* = *con-* (together) + *tenere* (to hold)
**例句**：This textbook **contains** all the essential grammar rules you need to know.

---

### 52. content
**音标**：/kənˈtent/
**词性**：adj./n.
**中文释义**：满足的，满意的；内容，目录
**英文释义**：satisfied with what one has; the subject matter of a text
**词根**：Latin *contentus* (satisfied) from *continere* (to hold together)
**例句**：She was **content** with her simple life in the countryside.

---

### 53. context
**音标**：/ˈkɒntekst/
**词性**：n.
**中文释义**：上下文，语境，背景
**英文释义**：the circumstances that form the setting for an event or idea
**词根**：Latin *contexere* = *con-* (together) + *texere* (to weave)
**例句**：You cannot understand the meaning of a word without considering its **context**.

---

### 54. continue
**音标**：/kənˈtɪnjuː/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：继续，延续，继续说
**英文释义**：to persist in an activity or state; to remain
**词根**：Latin *continuare* = *con-* (together) + *tenere* (to hold)
**例句**：Despite the challenges, she decided to **continue** her education abroad.

---

### 55. contribute
**音标**：/kənˈtrɪbjuːt/
**词性**：v.
**中文释义**：贡献，捐献，投稿
**英文释义**：to give something to help achieve a goal; to provide input
**词根**：Latin *contribuere* = *con-* (together) + *tribuere* (to give)
**例句**：Every team member can **contribute** their unique skills to the project.

---

### 56. control
**音标**：/kənˈtrəʊl/
**词性**：n./v.
**中文释义**：控制，支配，操控
**英文释义**：the power to influence behavior; to regulate or支配
**词根**：Latin *contra* (against) + *rotulus* (a little wheel) — originally to check by a counter
**例句**：The government has implemented new measures to **control** pollution.

---

### 57. convert

## Day 8
### 211. current
**音标**: /ˈkʌrənt/
**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 当前的；水流，电流，趋势
**英文释义**: belonging to the present time / a flow of water or electricity / a tendency
**词根**: Latin *currere* (to run) + *-ent*
**一词多义**:
- The current situation is concerning. (当前的局势令人担忧。)
- The river has a strong current. (这条河水流湍急。)
- Fashion trends reflect the current of the times. (时尚潮流反映时代趋势。)

---
### 212. novel
**音标**: /ˈnɒvəl/
**词性**: n./adj.
**中文释义**: 小说；新颖的，新奇的
**英文释义**: a book-length story / new and unusual
**词根**: Latin *novus* (new)
**一词多义**:
- She wrote her first novel at age 25. (她25岁时写了第一部小说。)
- That's a novel approach to the problem. (这是处理这个问题的一种新颖方法。)

---
### 213. bank
**音标**: /bæŋk/
**词性**: n./v.
**中文释义**: 银行；河岸；存入（银行）
**英文释义**: a financial institution / the side of a river / to deposit money
**词根**: Old Italian *banca* / Old Norse *bakki*
**一词多义**:
- I need to go to the bank to withdraw money. (我需要去银行取钱。)
- The river bank was covered with wild flowers. (河岸上开满了野花。)
- She banked her savings at the local branch. (她把积蓄存到了当地分行。)

---
### 214. spring
**音标**: /sprɪŋ/
**词性**: n./v.
**中文释义**: 春天；弹簧；泉水；跳跃
**英文释义**: the season / a coil of metal / a source of water / to jump
**词根**: Old English *spring*
**一词多义**:
- The trees bloom in spring. (树木在春天开花。)
- The mattress has a broken spring. (床垫有一个坏了的弹簧。)
- This area is known for its mineral springs. (这个地区以矿泉闻名。)
- The cat sprang onto the table. (猫跳到了桌子上。)

---
### 215. fair
**音标**: /feə/
**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 公平的；博览会，集市；美丽的
**英文释义**: just and impartial / a public event / beautiful
**词根**: Old English *fæger*
**一词多义**:
- It's not fair to blame only him. (只责怪他是不公平的。)
- The county fair features local crafts. (县博览会展示当地的手工艺品。)
- She has a fair complexion. (她有着白皙的肤色。)

---
### 216. firm

**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 坚固的；公司，商行
**英文释义**: solid and stable / a business company
**词根**: Latin *firmus*
**一词多义**:
- The foundation must be firm. (地基必须坚固。)
- He works for an accounting firm. (他在一家会计公司工作。)

---
### 217. proper

**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 适当的；严格的；本身的；正当的
**英文释义**: suitable / strict / belonging to something / correct
**词根**: Latin *proprius*

- Wear proper attire for the event. (请穿着适当的服装出席活动。)
- She keeps proper distance from colleagues. (她与同事保持严格的距离。)

### 218. present

**词性**: n./adj./v.
**中文释义**: 礼物；当前的；呈现，赠送
**英文释义**: a gift / existing now / to show or give
**词根**: Latin *praesens* / *praesentare*
**一词多义**:
- I bought a birthday present for my friend. (我给朋友买了一份生日礼物。)
- The present situation is challenging. (目前的形势很有挑战性。)
- She presented her research at the conference. (她在会议上呈现了她的研究。)

---
### 219. express
**音标**: /ɪkˈspres/
**词性**: v./adj./n.

- It's difficult to express my feelings. (很难表达我的感受。)
- I took the express train to Beijing. (我坐特快列车去北京。)
- We offer express delivery service. (我们提供快递服务。)

---
### 220. industry
**音标**: /ˈɪndəstri/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 工业；勤奋，勤劳

- His industry in studying is admirable. (他学习的勤奋令人钦佩。)

---
### 221. last
**音标**: /lɑːst/
**词性**: adj./adv./v.
**中文释义**: 最后的；持续；上次
**英文释义**: coming after all others / to continue / most recent
**词根**: Old English *latest*
**一词多义**:
- She was the last person to leave. (她是最后离开的人。)
- The meeting lasted three hours. (会议持续了三个小时。)
- I last saw him in Beijing. (我上次在北京见到他。)

### 222. just

**词性**: adj./adv.
**中文释义**: 公正的；刚才；只是，恰好
**英文释义**: fair and right / a short time ago / only, exactly
**词根**: Latin *justus*
**一词多义**:
- A just judge considers all evidence. (公正的法官会考虑所有证据。)

---
### 223. right

**词性**: adj./n./adv./v.

**英文释义**: correct / the right side / a moral or legal claim / to correct
**词根**: Old English *riht*

- You gave the right answer. (你给出了正确答案。)

- Please right the wrongs that were done. (请纠正所犯的错误。)

**一词多义**:
- She said she would come. (她说她会来。)
- I would appreciate your help. (我很感激你的帮助。)
- If I were you, I would accept the offer. (如果我是你，我会接受这个提议。)

---
### 230. should
**音标**: /ʃʊd/
**词性**: modal v.
**中文释义**: 应该；万一；竟然
**英文释义**: ought to / in case / expressing surprise
**词根**: Old English *sceolde*
**一词多义**:
- You should finish your homework first. (你应该先完成作业。)
- Should you need help, call me. (万一你需要帮助，打电话给我。)
- It's strange that you should say that. (你竟然说出那样的话，真奇怪。)

---
### 231. must
**音标**: /mʌst/
**词性**: modal v.
**中文释义**: 必须；偏偏；一定（表推测）
**英文释义**: to be obliged to / expressing annoyance / expressing certainty
**词根**: Old English *moste*
**一词多义**:
- You must complete this by Friday. (你必须在周五前完成这个。)
- Why must you always interrupt me? (你偏偏总是打断我？)
- He must be at home now. The lights are on. (他一定在家。灯亮着。)

---
### 232. can
**音标**: /kæn/
**词性**: modal v.
**中文释义**: 能；可能；罐装
**英文释义**: to be able to / to be possible / a container of metal
**词根**: Old English *cann*
**一词多义**:
- I can speak three languages. (我会说三种语言。)
- It can be dangerous to swim alone. (独自游泳可能有危险。)
- Bring a can of soda. (带一罐汽水来。)

---
### 233. may
**音标**: /meɪ/
**词性**: modal v./n.
**中文释义**: 可能；五月；祝愿
**英文释义**: expressing possibility / the fifth month / expressing a wish
**词根**: Old English *mæg* / Latin *Maius*
**一词多义**:
- It may rain later today. (今天稍后可能会下雨。)
- May I use your phone? (我可以用你的电话吗？)
- May you succeed in your new venture! (祝你在新事业中成功！)

---
### 234. will
**音标**: /wɪl/
**词性**: modal v./n.
**中文释义**: 将要；意志；遗嘱
**英文释义**: expressing future / determination / a legal document
**词根**: Old English *will*
**一词多义**:
- I will help you with the project. (我会帮你做这个项目。)
- She has a strong will to succeed. (她有强烈的成功意志。)
- His will states that everything goes to his children. (他的遗嘱规定所有财产都留给孩子。)

---
### 235. make
**音标**: /meɪk/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 制作；使成为；到达；理解
**英文释义**: to create / to cause to be / to reach / to interpret
**词根**: Old English *macian*
**一词多义**:
- She makes beautiful paintings. (她画美丽的画。)
- His achievements made him famous. (他的成就使他出名。)
- Can you make the deadline? (你能按时完成吗？)
- What do you make of this situation? (你对这情况怎么理解？)

---
### 236. get
**音标**: /ɡet/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 获得；到达；理解；使得
**英文释义**: to receive / to arrive / to understand / to cause
**词根**: Old English *gitan*
**一词多义**:
- I got a gift from my friend. (我收到了朋友送的礼物。)
- When will we get to the airport? (我们什么时候到机场？)
- I get what you're saying. (我理解你在说什么。)
- Get him to agree with the plan. (让他同意这个计划。)

---
### 237. go
**音标**: /ɡəʊ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 去；变为；处于；进行
**英文释义**: to move / to become / to be in a state / to proceed
**词根**: Old English *gan*
**一词多义**:
- Let's go to the park. (我们去公园吧。)
- The food has gone bad. (食物变质了。)
- Things go well with me. (我一切顺利。)
- The meeting went smoothly. (会议进行得很顺利。)

---
### 238. come
**音标**: /kʌm/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 来；成为；到达
**英文释义**: to move toward / to become / to arrive
**词根**: Old English *cuman*
**一词多义**:
- Come here please. (请过来。)
- The button came loose. (按钮松了。)
- The deadline has come. (截止日期到了。)

---
### 239. see
**音标**: /siː/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 看见；理解；确保；经历
**英文释义**: to look at / to understand / to make sure / to experience
**词根**: Old English *seon*
**一词多义**:
- I can't see without my glasses. (我不戴眼镜看不见。)
- I see your point. (我理解你的观点。)
- See that the door is locked. (确保门锁好了。)
- She saw many changes in her lifetime. (她经历了许多变迁。)

---
### 240. run
**音标**: /rʌn/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 跑；运行；经营；变成
**英文释义**: to move quickly / to operate / to manage / to become
**词根**: Old English *rinnan*
**一词多义**:
- I run every morning for exercise. (我每天早上跑步锻炼。)
- The computer runs smoothly. (电脑运行流畅。)
- They run a small restaurant. (他们经营一家小餐馆。)
- The river ran dry during summer. (夏天河干了。)

## Day 9
### 241. take
**音标**: /teɪk/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 拿；带走；花费；认为
**英文释义**: to seize / to carry / to require / to consider
**词根**: Old English *tacian*
**一词多义**:
- Take this book to the library. (把这本书带到图书馆去。)
- It takes two hours to get there. (到那里需要两个小时。)
- I take it as a compliment. (我认为那是恭维。)
- Take your time, there's no rush. (慢慢来,不用急。)

---
### 242. put
**音标**: /pʊt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 放；使处于；表达；评价
**英文释义**: to place / to cause to be / to express / to estimate
**词根**: Old English *putian*
**一词多义**:
- Put the book on the table. (把书放在桌子上。)
- Put the light on. (把灯打开。)
- How would you put this in English? (这个用英语怎么说？)
- I'd put her among the best singers. (我会把她列为最好的歌手之一。)

---
### 243. give
**音标**: /ɡɪv/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 给；产生；举办；授予
**英文释义**: to present / to produce / to organize / to confer
**词根**: Old English *giefan*
**一词多义**:
- Give me a cup of water please. (请给我一杯水。)
- The sun gives warmth to the earth. (太阳给地球温暖。)
- They gave a party for her birthday. (他们为她的生日举办了一个聚会。)
- The university gave him an honorary degree. (大学授予他荣誉学位。)

---
### 244. look
**音标**: /lʊk/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 看；看起来；查找；留神
**英文释义**: to use one's eyes / to appear / to search / to pay attention
**词根**: Old English *locian*
**一词多义**:
- Look at the blackboard please. (请看黑板。)
- She looks very tired today. (她今天看起来很疲倦。)
- I'm looking for my keys. (我在找我的钥匙。)
- Look before you leap. (三思而后行。)

---
### 245. think
**音标**: /θɪŋk/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 想；认为；考虑；想要
**英文释义**: to use one's mind / to believe / to consider / to intend
**词根**: Old English *þencan*
**一词多义**:
- I think it's going to rain. (我认为天要下雨了。)
- Think before you speak. (说话前先思考。)
- I'm thinking of studying abroad. (我正在考虑出国学习。)
- He thinks highly of her work. (他对她的工作评价很高。)

---
### 246. use
**音标**: /juːz/ /juːs/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 使用；利用；对待；用途
**英文释义**: to employ / to take advantage of / to act toward / purpose
**词根**: Old English *usian*
**一词多义**:
- May I use your phone? (我可以用你的电话吗？)
- We should use this opportunity. (我们应该利用这个机会。)
- He used me for his own purposes. (他利用我达到自己的目的。)
- This tool has many uses. (这个工具有很多用途。)

---
### 247. find
**音标**: /faɪnd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 发现；找到；感到；判决
**英文释义**: to discover / to locate / to feel / to decide
**词根**: Old English *findan*
**一词多义**:
- I found my lost wallet. (我找到了丢失的钱包。)
- I find it difficult to learn Chinese. (我觉得学中文很难。)
- The court found him guilty. (法庭判决他有罪。)
- You'll find a way, I believe. (我相信你会找到办法的。)

---
### 248. tell
**音标**: /tel/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 告诉；分辨；命令；显示
**英文释义**: to communicate / to distinguish / to order / to indicate
**词根**: Old English *tellan*
**一词多义**:
- Tell me a story please. (请给我讲个故事。)
- Can you tell the difference between these two? (你能分辨出这两者的区别吗？)
- Tell him to wait outside. (让他在外面等。)
- The results tell us something important. (结果显示了一些重要的东西。)

---
### 249. say
**音标**: /seɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 说；表达；比如说；据说
**英文释义**: to speak / to convey / to give as example / reported
**词根**: Old English *secgan*
**一词多义**:
- What did you say? (你说什么？)
- It is said that practice makes perfect. (据说熟能生巧。)
- Let's say you have one million dollars. (比如说,你有一百万美元。)
- She said nothing about the incident. (她对这件事什么都没说。)

---
### 250. keep
**音标**: /kiːp/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 保持；保留；继续；遵守
**英文释义**: to hold / to retain / to continue / to obey
**词根**: Old English *cepan*
**一词多义**:
- Keep quiet in the library. (在图书馆保持安静。)
- You can keep the book for a week. (这本书你可以借一周。)
- Keep working hard and you'll succeed. (继续努力，你会成功的。)
- Everyone must keep the rules. (每个人都必须遵守规则。)

---
### 251. let
**音标**: /let/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 让；假设；出租；让某人做
**英文释义**: to allow / to suppose / to rent / to cause to do
**词根**: Old English *letan*
**一词多义**:
- Let me help you with that. (让我帮你做这个。)
- Let ABC be equal to XYZ. (假设ABC等于XYZ。)
- They let out their spare room. (他们把多余的房间出租了。)
- Don't let him take advantage of you. (别让他占你便宜。)

---
### 252. begin
**音标**: /bɪˈɡɪn/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 开始；着手
**英文释义**: to start / to commence
**词根**: Old English *beginnan*
**一词多义**:
- Let's begin the meeting now. (让我们现在开始开会。)
- She began to understand the situation. (她开始明白情况了。)
- The concert begins at 8 pm. (音乐会晚上8点开始。)
- Knowledge begins with practice. (认识从实践开始。)

---
### 253. seem
**音标**: /siːm/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 似乎；看起来；好像
**英文释义**: to appear / to look / to give the impression
**词根**: Old English *seem*
**一词多义**:
- You seem happy today. (你今天看起来很开心。)
- It seems that it will rain. (好像要下雨了。)
- He seems like a nice person. (他看起来像个好人。)
- What seems to be the problem? (问题出在哪里？)

---
### 254. help
**音标**: /help/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 帮助；帮忙；对…有帮助
**英文释义**: to assist / aid / to be useful
**词根**: Old English *helpan*
**一词多义**:
- Can you help me with this problem? (你能帮我解决这个问题吗？)
- Thank you for your help. (谢谢你的帮助。)
- This information might help. (这个信息可能有用。)
- She helped him find his way. (她帮他找到了路。)

---
### 255. show
**音标**: /ʃəʊ/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 显示；表明；证明；展示
**英文释义**: to display / to indicate / to prove / an exhibition
**词根**: Old English *sweawian*
**一词多义**:
- Show me your ID please. (请给我看你的身份证。)
- The data shows a clear trend. (数据显示出明显的趋势。)
- This proves it, you see. (你看，这证明了这一点。)
- There's a flower show this weekend. (这个周末有花展。)

---
### 256. try
**音标**: /traɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 尝试；努力；审问；试用
**英文释义**: to attempt / to make an effort / to test in court / to test
**词根**: Old English *treowan*
**一词多义**:
- Let me try again. (让我再试一次。)
- Try your best to finish on time. (尽力按时完成。)
- They tried him for theft. (他们以盗窃罪审问他。)
- You can try this dress on. (你可以试穿这条裙子。)

---
### 257. leave
**音标**: /liːv/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 离开；留下；导致；遗留
**英文释义**: to depart / to allow to remain / to cause / to bequeath
**词根**: Old English *lefian*
**一词多义**:
- It's time to leave. (该离开了。)
- Leave the door open please. (让门开着吧。)
- The storm left a lot of damage. (暴风雨造成了很多损害。)
- He left all his property to charity. (他把所有财产都遗赠给了慈善机构。)

---
### 258. call
**音标**: /kɔːl/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 呼叫；称呼；打电话；召集
**英文释义**: to shout / to name / to telephone / to summon
**词根**: Old English *ceallian*
**一词多义**:
- Call me when you arrive. (到了就给我打电话。)
- What do you call this in English? (这个用英语怎么说？)
- The meeting has been called for 3 pm. (会议定在下午3点。)
- She heard a call for help. (她听到了求救声。)

---
### 259. turn
**音标**: /tɜːn/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 转向；转变；旋转；轮班
**英文释义**: to change direction / to transform / to rotate / a shift
**词根**: Old English *turnian*
**一词多义**:
- Turn left at the corner. (在拐角处左转。)
- The weather has turned cold. (天气变冷了。)
- Turn the key in the lock. (把钥匙转动开锁。)
- It's your turn to clean up. (轮到你打扫了。)

---
### 260. need
**音标**: /niːd/
**词性**: v./modal/n.
**中文释义**: 需要；必须；需要的东西
**英文释义**: to require / to be obliged / something required
**词根**: Old English *nied*
**一词多义**:
- I need your help. (我需要你的帮助。)
- Need I remind you of your duties? (需要我提醒你的职责吗？)
- There's a great need for teachers. (非常需要教师。)
- We need to act now. (我们需要现在就行动。)

---
### 261. become
**音标**: /bɪˈkʌm/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 变成；成为；适合
**英文释义**: to grow to be / to come to be / to suit
**词根**: Old English *becuman*
**一词多义**:
- She wants to become a doctor. (她想成为一名医生。)
- It became clear that he was lying. (很明显他在撒谎。)
- This coat becomes you well. (这件外套很适合你。)
- The situation is becoming serious. (情况正在变得严重。)

---
### 262. lead
**音标**: /liːd/ /led/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 引导；领导；导致；铅
**英文释义**: to guide / to be in charge / to result in / a metal
**词根**: Old English *laedan*
**一词多义**:
- Follow me, I'll lead the way. (跟我走,我来带路。)
- She leads the marketing team. (她领导市场营销团队。)
- Smoking leads to many health problems. (吸烟导致很多健康问题。)
- This pipe is made of lead. (这根管子是铅做的。)

---
### 263. understand
**音标**: /ˌʌndəˈstænd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 理解；懂；得知；以为
**英文释义**: to comprehend / to know / to learn / to interpret
**词根**: Old English *understandan*
**一词多义**:
- I don't understand what you mean. (我不明白你的意思。)
- Do you understand English? (你懂英语吗？)
- I understood from her that you were coming. (我从她那得知你要来。)
- What I understood was different. (我以为的是不一样的。)

---
### 264. write
**音标**: /raɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 写；写作；填写；导致
**英文释义**: to form letters / to compose / to fill in / to cause
**词根**: Old English *writan*
**一词多义**:
- Please write your name here. (请在这里写下你的名字。)
- She writes for a newspaper. (她为一家报纸撰稿。)
- Write 'N/A' if not applicable. (如果不适用,填写'N/A'。)
- His absence wrote itself into history. (他的缺席被载入了史册。)

---
### 265. provide
**音标**: /prəˈvaɪd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 提供；装备；供养；规定
**英文释义**: to supply / to equip / to support / to stipulate
**词根**: Latin *providere*
**一词多义**:
- The hotel provides free breakfast. (酒店提供免费早餐。)
- They provided us with everything we needed. (他们为我们提供了一切需要的东西。)
- He has a family to provide for. (他有家庭要供养。)
- The law provides for equal treatment. (法律规定了平等待遇。)

---
### 266. sit
**音标**: /sɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 坐；使坐；开会；位于
**英文释义**: to rest on a seat / to place / to hold a meeting / to be located
**词根**: Old English *sittan*
**一词多义**:
- Please sit down. (请坐下。)
- Sit the baby in the chair. (把婴儿放在椅子上坐好。)
- The court sits in this building. (法院在这座楼里开庭。)
- The hotel sits on the hill. (酒店位于山上。)

---
### 267. stand
**音标**: /stænd/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 站；使站立；位于；停滞
**英文释义**: to be upright / to place / to be located / to remain
**词根**: Old English *standan*
**一词多义**:
- Stand up please. (请站起来。)
- I can't stand this heat anymore. (我再也受不了这种热度了。)
- The building stands at the corner. (那栋楼位于拐角处。)
- There was a stand-off between the two sides. (双方陷入了僵局。)

---
### 268. lose
**音标**: /luːz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 丢失；输掉；迷失；错过
**英文释义**: to misplace / to fail to win / to become astray / to miss
**词根**: Old English *losian*
**一词多义**:
- I lost my keys again. (我又丢了钥匙。)
- Our team lost the game. (我们队输了比赛。)
- The child got lost in the forest. (孩子在森林里迷路了。)
- You lose the opportunity if you hesitate. (如果你犹豫就错过了机会。)

---
### 269. pay
**音标**: /peɪ/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 支付；偿还；有益于；工资
**英文释义**: to give money / to repay / to benefit / wages
**词根**: Old English *pagian*
**一词多义**:
- I need to pay the bill. (我需要付账单。)
- It pays to be honest. (诚实是有益的。)
- Pay attention to the teacher. (注意听老师讲课。)
- He received his pay for the month. (他领到了这个月的工资。)

---
### 270. meet
**音标**: /miːt/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 遇见；满足；开会；遭遇
**英文释义**: to come face to face / to satisfy / to hold a meeting / to encounter
**词根**: Old English *metan*
**一词多义**:
- Nice to meet you. (很高兴认识你。)
- This meeting will be held next week. (会议将在下周举行。)
- We must meet the deadline. (我们必须满足最后期限。)
- He met an old friend on the street. (他在街上遇到了一位老朋友。)

## Day 10
### 271. include
**音标**: /ɪnˈkluːd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 包括；包含；算入
**英文释义**: to contain / to comprise / to count in
**词根**: Latin *includere*
**一词多义**:
- The price includes tax. (价格包含税。)
- Your duties include cleaning. (你的职责包括打扫卫生。)
- Please include me in your plans. (请把我也算进你的计划里。)

---
### 272. continue
**音标**: /kənˈtɪnjuː/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 继续；持续；维持
**英文释义**: to go on / to last / to maintain
**词根**: Latin *continuare*
**一词多义**:
- Continue working while I was away. (我不在时请继续工作。)
- The rain continued for three days. (雨连续下了三天。)
- We must continue to fight for our rights. (我们必须继续为权利而战。)

---
### 273. change
**音标**: /tʃeɪndʒ/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 改变；交换；兑换；变化
**英文释义**: to make different / to exchange / to give different denominations / alteration
**词根**: Old French *changier*
**一词多义**:
- Things change over time. (事情随着时间而改变。)
- Can I change seats with you? (我能和你换座位吗？)
- I need to change dollars for RMB. (我需要把美元兑换成人民币。)
- There was a big change in plans. (计划有了很大的变化。)

---
### 274. explain
**音标**: /ɪkˈspleɪn/

**英文释义**: to make clear / to elucidate / to defend

**一词多义**:
- Can you explain this problem? (你能解释这个问题吗？)
- Please explain your answer. (请说明你的答案。)
- Explain why you were late. (解释一下你为什么迟到。)

---
### 275. develop
**音标**: /dɪˈveləp/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 发展；开发；冲洗；患
**英文释义**: to grow / to create / to process film / to contract

**一词多义**:
- We need to develop new skills. (我们需要发展新技能。)
- They developed a new software. (他们开发了一款新软件。)
- This photo needs to be developed. (这张照片需要冲洗。)
- He developed a cold last week. (他上周感冒了。)

---
### 276. bring

- The medicine brought relief. (这药带来了 relief。)
- What brought you to this city? (什么原因让你来到这个城市？)
- The rain brought mud into the house. (雨水把泥带进了屋子。)

### 277. work

- This is a great work of art. (这是一幅伟大的艺术作品。)

### 282. hold

- This room can hold 50 people. (这个房间能容纳50人。)

### 283. happen
**音标**: /ˈhæpən/

- Accidents will happen. (事故是难免的。)

### 284. produce
**音标**: /prəˈdjuːs/

**英文释义**: to make / to cause / to create / to show

- She produced evidence from her bag. (她从包里拿出了证据。)

### 285. result

### 286. create
**音标**: /kriˈeɪt/

### 287. expect

### 288. wish

### 289. ask
**音标**: /ɑːsk/

- The job asks for experience. (这份工作要求有经验。)
- They asked us to dinner. (他们邀请我们吃晚饭。)

---
### 290. need
**音标**: /niːd/

- There's no need to worry. (没必要担心。)

### 291. want

**词根**: Old English *wanian*
**一词多义**:
- I want to go home. (我想回家。)
- The house wants painting. (房子需要刷漆。)
- They are in want of food. (他们缺少食物。)
- He has a strong want for adventure. (他有强烈的冒险欲望。)

### 292. offer

**词性**: v./n.

**英文释义**: to present / to propose / to bid / to provide
**词根**: Latin *offere*
**一词多义**:

- I offered 500 dollars for it. (我出价500美元买这个。)
- He offered to help. (他主动提出帮忙。)
- The job offer is very attractive. (这份工作邀请很有吸引力。)

### 293. force

**词性**: v./n.

**英文释义**: to compel / to push / power / physical strength
**词根**: Latin *fortis*
**一词多义**:
- Don't force me to do this. (别强迫我做这个。)
- The wind forced the door open. (风把门吹开了。)
- The police used force to control the crowd. (警察用武力控制了人群。)
- Knowledge is power. (知识就是力量。)

### 294. maintain

**英文释义**: to keep / to preserve / to assert / to support
**词根**: Latin *manutenere*
**一词多义**:

- Maintain your composure. (保持镇定。)
- He maintains that he is innocent. (他坚持自己是清白的。)
- They maintain a family of six. (他们赡养一个六口之家。)

### 295. consider
**音标**: /kənˈsɪdə/

**词根**: Latin *considerare*
**一词多义**:
- Consider your options carefully. (仔细考虑你的选择。)
- I consider him a good friend. (我认为他是一个好朋友。)
- Please consider the feelings of others. (请体谅他人的感受。)
- He stood there considering the painting. (他站在那里注视着那幅画。)

---
### 296. sense
**音标**: /sens/
**词性**: v./n.

**英文释义**: to perceive / perception / meaning / a faculty
**词根**: Latin *sensus*
**一词多义**:
- I sensed something was wrong. (我感觉到有什么不对劲。)
- The phrase doesn't make sense. (这个短语没有意义。)
- Common sense told me to leave. (常识告诉我应该离开。)
- The five senses are sight, smell, taste, touch, hearing. (五种感官是视觉、嗅觉、味觉、触觉和听觉。)

### 297. appear
**音标**: /əˈpɪə/
**词性**: v.

**英文释义**: to come into view / to seem / to publish / to attend court
**词根**: Latin *apparere*
**一词多义**:
- A figure appeared in the distance. (远处出现了一个人影。)
- She appears very confident. (她显得很有信心。)
- The book will appear next month. (这本书下个月出版。)
- He appeared in court yesterday. (他昨天出庭了。)

---
### 298. decide
**音标**: /dɪˈsaɪd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 决定；判决；解决
**英文释义**: to make a choice / to judge / to resolve
**词根**: Latin *decidere*
**一词多义**:
- I haven't decided yet. (我还没决定。)
- We decided to go there. (我们决定去那里。)
- The judge will decide the case. (法官将判决这个案子。)
- This issue needs to be decided soon. (这个问题需要尽快解决。)

---
### 299. reach
**音标**: /riːtʃ/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 到达；伸手；联系；范围
**英文释义**: to arrive at / to extend / to contact / extent
**词根**: Old English *raecan*
**一词多义**:
- We reached Beijing yesterday. (我们昨天到达北京。)
- Please reach for the book on the shelf. (请伸手去够架子上的书。)
- You can reach me at this number. (你可以用这个号码联系我。)
- The deal is within reach. (这笔交易是可以实现的。)

---
### 300. receive
**音标**: /rɪˈsiːv/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 收到；接待；遭受；容纳
**英文释义**: to get / to welcome / to experience / to hold
**词根**: Latin *recipere*
**一词多义**:
- I received your letter yesterday. (我昨天收到了你的信。)
- The hotel receives many guests. (这家酒店接待许多客人。)
- He received a heavy blow. (他遭受了重重的一击。)
- The stadium can receive 50,000 people. (这个体育场能容纳五万人。)

## Day 11
### 301. describe

**词性**: v.

### 302. require

**英文释义**: to need / to demand / to order

### 303. report

**英文释义**: to give an account / to cover / to inform on / a record

- Report to the office at 9 am. (上午9点到办公室报到。)
- The newspaper reported the accident. (报纸报道了这起事故。)

- He wrote a report on the project. (他写了一份关于这个项目的报告。)

---
### 304. determine

- His actions determined his fate. (他的行为决定了他的命运。)

---
### 305. seem

### 306. cover

**英文释义**: to lie over / to hide / to report on / a front

- The book has a beautiful cover. (这本书有一个漂亮的封面。)

### 307. increase

- She raised three children alone. (她独自抚养了三个孩子。)

### 309. rise

- I have a great deal of work to do. (我有许多工作要做。)

### 311. matter

### 312. kill
**音标**: /kɪl/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 杀死；消磨；破坏；使痛苦
**英文释义**: to cause death / to pass time / to ruin / to hurt
**词根**: Middle English *killen*
**一词多义**:
- The frost killed the crops. (霜冻冻死了庄稼。)
- We killed time waiting for the bus. (我们等公共汽车消磨时间。)
- That joke killed me. (那个笑话笑死我了。)
- My feet are killing me. (我的脚痛死了。)

---
### 313. remain
**音标**: /rɪˈmeɪn/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 保持；仍然是；留下；剩余
**英文释义**: to stay / to continue to be / to be left / to be left over
**词根**: Latin *manere*
**一词多义**:
- Please remain seated. (请保持坐着。)
- The situation remains unchanged. (情况保持不变。)
- A few books remained on the shelf. (架子上还剩几本书。)
- What remains of the money? (还剩下多少钱？)

---
### 314. cause
**音标**: /kɔːz/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 导致；原因；事业
**英文释义**: to produce / an origin / a cause
**词根**: Latin *causa*
**一词多义**:
- Smoking causes cancer. (吸烟致癌。)
- What was the cause of the accident? (事故的原因是什么？)
- She fought for the cause of freedom. (她为自由事业而战。)

---
### 315. form
**音标**: /fɔːm/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 形成；构成；形式；形状
**英文释义**: to shape / to constitute / a shape / appearance
**词根**: Latin *forma*
**一词多义**:
- Water forms ice when cold. (水冷时形成冰。)
- Form good habits early. (早点养成好习惯。)
- There are many forms of government. (政府有多种形式。)
- The sculpture took the form of a bird. (雕塑呈鸟的形状。)

---
### 316. support
**音标**: /səˈpɔːt/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 支持；支撑；资助；证明
**英文释义**: to hold up / to back / to fund / to prove
**词根**: Latin *supportare*
**一词多义**:
- Support the government. (支持政府。)
- The pillar supports the roof. (柱子支撑着屋顶。)
- He supports his family. (他供养家人。)
- There is no evidence to support this claim. (没有证据支持这个说法。)

---
### 317. accept
**音标**: /əkˈsept/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 接受；承认；相信；容纳
**英文释义**: to receive / to admit / to believe / to contain
**词根**: Latin *acceptare*
**一词多义**:
- Please accept my apology. (请接受我的道歉。)
- I cannot accept your terms. (我不能接受你的条件。)
- The theory is widely accepted. (这个理论被广泛接受。)
- The city can accept more refugees. (这座城市能容纳更多难民。)

---
### 318. cut
**音标**: /kʌt/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 切；割；削减；伤口
**英文释义**: to divide / to reduce / a wound
**词根**: Middle English *cutten*
**一词多义**:
- Cut the cake into slices. (把蛋糕切成片。)
- They cut the budget. (他们削减了预算。)
- He had a cut on his finger. (他手指上有一道伤口。)
- Power cuts happened during the storm. (暴风雨期间发生了停电。)

---
### 319. hit
**音标**: /hɪt/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 打；碰撞；命中；成功
**英文释义**: to strike / to collide / a successful move
**词根**: Middle English *hitten*
**一词多义**:
- Hit the ball hard. (用力击球。)
- The car hit the wall. (汽车撞到了墙上。)
- The movie was a big hit. (这部电影大获成功。)
- That's a hit song. (那是一首热门歌曲。)

---
### 320. watch
**音标**: /wɒtʃ/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 观看；注视；看守；手表
**英文释义**: to look at / to observe / to guard / a timepiece
**词根**: Old English *waecce*
**一词多义**:
- Watch the movie carefully. (仔细观看这部电影。)
- Watch what I do. (注意看我的动作。)
- The guard watches the building. (警卫看守着那栋楼。)
- I wear a watch. (我戴手表。)

---
### 321. serve
**音标**: /sɜːv/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 服务；任职；供应；对待
**英文释义**: to work for / to hold office / to provide / to treat
**词根**: Old English *servian*
**一词多义**:
- She serves as a teacher. (她担任教师。)
- The restaurant serves lunch. (这家餐厅供应午餐。)
- He served the customers well. (他对待顾客很好。)
- The soldier served his country. (士兵为国效力。)

---
### 322. seek
**音标**: /siːk/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 寻找；寻求；追求
**英文释义**: to look for / to try to obtain / to pursue
**词根**: Old English *secan*
**一词多义**:
- Seek help when you need it. (需要帮助时请寻求帮助。)
- They seek a peaceful solution. (他们寻求和平解决方案。)
- He seeks fame and fortune. (他追求名利。)
- The man is seeking employment. (那个人在找工作。)

---
### 323. involve
**音标**: /ɪnˈvɒlv/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 包含；使参与；使牵连
**英文释义**: to include / to engage / to implicate
**词根**: Latin *involvere*
**一词多义**:
- The project involves a lot of work. (这个项目包含很多工作。)
- Don't involve me in your problems. (别让我卷入你的问题中。)
- The accident involved two trucks. (事故涉及两辆卡车。)

---
### 324. speak
**音标**: /spiːk/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 说话；发言；说(语言)；表明
**英文释义**: to talk / to address / to use a language / to indicate
**词根**: Old English *sprecan*
**一词多义**:
- Can you speak English? (你会说英语吗？)
- She spoke at the conference. (她在会议上发言。)
- Actions speak louder than words. (行动胜于言语。)

---
### 325. pay
**音标**: /peɪ/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 支付；偿还；工资；有益
**英文释义**: to give money / to repay / wages / benefit
**词根**: Old English *pagian*
**一词多义**:
- Pay the bill please. (请付账。)
- Pay attention in class. (上课要注意听讲。)
- His pay is very low. (他的工资很低。)
- It pays to be honest. (诚实是有益的。)

---
### 326. spend
**音标**: /spend/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 花费；度过；消耗；用尽
**英文释义**: to use money / to pass time / to use up / to exhaust
**词根**: Old English *spendan*
**一词多义**:
- I spent 100 dollars on this. (我在这上面花了100美元。)
- I spent the weekend in Beijing. (我在北京度过了周末。)
- The storm spent its force. (暴风雨失去了力量。)

---
### 327. succeed
**音标**: /səkˈsiːd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 成功；继承；接着…发生
**英文释义**: to achieve / to inherit / to follow
**词根**: Latin *succedere*
**一词多义**:
- You can succeed if you try hard. (努力就能成功。)
- She succeeded her father as manager. (她接替父亲担任经理。)
- What succeeded the Roman Empire? (罗马帝国之后是什么？)

---
### 328. grow
**音标**: /ɡrəʊ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 成长；增长；变得；种植
**英文释义**: to develop / to increase / to become / to cultivate
**词根**: Old English *growan*
**一词多义**:
- Children grow quickly. (孩子们成长很快。)
- The company is growing. (公司在增长。)
- It's growing dark. (天渐渐黑了。)
- They grow vegetables in the garden. (他们在花园里种植蔬菜。)

---
### 329. feel
**音标**: /fiːl/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 感觉；触摸；认为；感受
**英文释义**: to sense / to touch / to think / an impression
**词根**: Old English *felan*
**一词多义**:
- I feel happy today. (我今天感觉很开心。)
- Feel the texture of the cloth. (摸摸这块布的质地。)
- I feel like having some ice cream. (我想吃点冰淇淋。)
- The feel of the room was cozy. (房间的感觉很温馨。)

---
### 330. fall
**音标**: /fɔːl/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 落下；下降；倒下；秋天
**英文释义**: to drop / to decline / to collapse / autumn
**词根**: Old English *feallan*
**一词多义**:
- Leaves fall in autumn. (秋天树叶落下。)
- The temperature fell sharply. (温度急剧下降。)
- She fell down the stairs. (她从楼梯上摔了下来。)
- Fall is my favorite season. (秋天是我最喜欢的季节。)

## Day 12
### 331. buy
**音标**: /baɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 买；收买；相信
**英文释义**: to purchase / to bribe / to accept
**词根**: Old English *bycgan*
**一词多义**:
- I want to buy a new car. (我想买一辆新车。)
- You can't buy happiness. (钱买不到幸福。)
- He was bought off by the enemy. (他被敌人收买了。)
- I don't buy his explanation. (我不相信他的解释。)

---
### 332. wait
**音标**: /weɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 等待；伺候；期待
**英文释义**: to stay / to serve / to look forward to
**词根**: Old English *wacian*
**一词多义**:
- Wait for me please. (请等我。)
- She waits on the customers. (她招待顾客。)
- I can't wait to see you. (我迫不及待想见你。)

---
### 333. lie
**音标**: /laɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 躺；说谎；位于；处于
**英文释义**: to be in horizontal position / to tell falsehoods / to be situated / to be in a state
**词根**: Old English *licgan*
**一词多义**:
- The cat is lying on the bed. (猫正躺在床上。)
- Don't lie to me. (别对我说谎。)
- The town lies in a valley. (小镇位于山谷中。)
- The problem lies in the design. (问题在于设计。)

---
### 334. die
**音标**: /daɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 死；灭亡；渴望
**英文释义**: to cease living / to perish / to desire intensely
**词根**: Old English *diegan*
**一词多义**:
- He died in 1999. (他于1999年去世。)
- The flowers are dying. (花正在枯萎。)
- I'm dying for a cup of coffee. (我非常想喝杯咖啡。)

---
### 335. wear
**音标**: /weə/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 穿；佩戴；磨损；面容
**英文释义**: to have on / to carry on body / to damage / appearance
**词根**: Old English *werian*
**一词多义**:
- She wears a red dress. (她穿着一条红色的裙子。)
- I wear glasses. (我戴眼镜。)
- The steps are worn smooth. (台阶磨损得很光滑。)
- He had a tired wear on his face. (他面带倦容。)

---
### 336. eat
**音标**: /iːt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 吃；腐蚀
**英文释义**: to consume food / to corrode
**词根**: Old English *etan*
**一词多义**:
- We eat three meals a day. (我们每天吃三顿饭。)
- Acid eats metal. (酸腐蚀金属。)
- Eat your vegetables. (把蔬菜吃掉。)

---
### 337. draw
**音标**: /drɔː/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 画；拉；抽出；提取
**英文释义**: to make a picture / to pull / to take out / to withdraw
**词根**: Old English *dragan*
**一词多义**:
- Draw a picture for me. (给我画一幅画。)
- Draw the curtain please. (请拉上窗帘。)
- Draw money from the bank. (从银行取钱。)
- The game is a draw. (比赛平局了。)

---
### 338. choose
**音标**: /tʃuːz/
**词性**: v.

**英文释义**: to select / to decide
**词根**: Old English *ceosan*
**一词多义**:

- There's nothing to choose between them. (两者没什么可挑的。)

### 339. contain
**音标**: /kənˈteɪn/

**英文释义**: to hold / to include / to control

**一词多义**:

- The bottle contains water. (瓶子里装着水。)

### 340. study
**音标**: /ˈstʌdi/
**词性**: v./n.

**英文释义**: to learn / to investigate / a room for reading

- The professor has a small study. (教授有一间小书房。)

### 341. stop
**音标**: /stɒp/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 停止；阻止；车站
**英文释义**: to cease / to prevent / a station
**词根**: Middle English *stoppen*
**一词多义**:
- Stop talking please. (请停止说话。)
- Nothing can stop us now. (现在什么也阻止不了我们。)
- I get off at the next stop. (我在下一站下车。)

### 342. learn
**音标**: /lɜːn/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 学习；得知；记住

- Learn the poem by heart. (记住这首诗。)

### 343. add
**音标**: /æd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 添加；补充；加起来

### 344. open

### 345. walk
**音标**: /wɔːk/

### 346. win
**音标**: /wɪn/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 赢；获得；赶上

### 347. teach
**音标**: /tiːtʃ/

**中文释义**: 教；教导；教训

### 348. allow

**中文释义**: 喜欢；享受；享有
**英文释义**: to like / to take pleasure in / to have
**词根**: Old French *enjoir*
**一词多义**:
- I enjoy playing tennis. (我喜欢打网球。)
- Enjoy your meal. (请慢用。)
- The town enjoys good weather. (这个镇享有好天气。)

---
### 357. hope
**音标**: /həʊp/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 希望；期望
**英文释义**: to wish for / to expect
**词根**: Old English *hopian*
**一词多义**:
- I hope you are well. (我希望你很好。)
- We hope to see you soon. (我们希望很快见到你。)
- There is still hope. (还有希望。)

---
### 358. call
**音标**: /kɔːl/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 打电话；叫；称呼；召集
**英文释义**: to telephone / to cry out / to name / to summon
**词根**: Old English *ceallian*
**一词多义**:
- I'll call you tonight. (我今晚会给你打电话。)
- The teacher called my name. (老师叫了我的名字。)
- They call the city Paris. (他们称这座城市为巴黎。)
- The meeting was called off. (会议被取消了。)

---
### 359. trust
**音标**: /trʌst/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 信任；相信；委托
**英文释义**: to believe / to have faith / to entrust
**词根**: Old Norse *traust*
**一词多义**:
- I trust you completely. (我完全信任你。)
- Don't trust strangers. (不要相信陌生人。)
- She was betrayed by those she trusted. (她被信任的人出卖了。)

---
### 360. hope
**音标**: /həʊp/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 希望；盼望
**英文释义**: to wish / to look forward to
**词根**: Old English *hopian*
**一词多义**:
- I hope it won't rain. (我希望不要下雨。)
- We hope for the best. (我们抱最好的希望。)
- There is no hope left. (没有希望了。)

## Day 13
### 361. concern
**音标**: /kənˈsɜːn/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 关心；涉及；使担心
**英文释义**: to worry / to relate to / to make anxious
**词根**: Latin *concernere*
**一词多义**:
- This concerns your future. (这关系到你的未来。)
- I'm concerned about her health. (我担心她的健康。)
- The letter was marked 'Private and Confidential'. (信件标注了"私人机密"。)

---
### 362. appear
**音标**: /əˈpɪə/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 出现；看来；似乎；出版
**英文释义**: to come into view / to seem / to be published
**词根**: Latin *apparere*
**一词多义**:
- A ghost appeared in the mirror. (镜子里出现了一个鬼。)
- You appear tired today. (你今天看起来很累。)
- The book will appear next month. (这本书下个月出版。)

---
### 363. decide
**音标**: /dɪˈsaɪd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 决定；判决；解决
**英文释义**: to make a choice / to settle by judgment / to resolve
**词根**: Latin *decidere*
**一词多义**:
- Decide on a plan. (决定一个计划。)
- The judge decided the case. (法官判决了这个案件。)
- We need to decide this matter. (我们需要解决这个问题。)

---
### 364. suggest
**音标**: /səˈdʒest/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 建议；暗示；启发
**英文释义**: to propose / to hint / to inspire
**词根**: Latin *suggerere*
**一词多义**:
- I suggest you take a break. (我建议你休息一下。)
- Her expression suggested she was unhappy. (她的表情暗示她不开心。)
- The name suggests its meaning. (这个名字暗示了它的含义。)

---
### 365. require
**音标**: /rɪˈkwaɪə/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 需要；要求；命令
**英文释义**: to need / to demand / to order
**词根**: Latin *requirere*
**一词多义**:
- The job requires a college degree. (这份工作需要大学学位。)
- Students are required to attend class. (学生必须上课。)
- The law requires you to pay taxes. (法律规定你必须纳税。)

---
### 366. report
**音标**: /rɪˈpɔːt/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 报告；报道；举报
**英文释义**: to give an account / to cover / to inform against
**词根**: Latin *reportare*
**一词多义**:
- Report any problems to me. (有任何问题向我报告。)
- The newspaper reported the event. (报纸报道了这个事件。)
- They reported him to the authorities. (他们向当局举报了他。)

---
### 367. consider
**音标**: /kənˈsɪdə/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 考虑；认为；关心
**英文释义**: to think about / to regard / to show consideration
**词根**: Latin *considerare*
**一词多义**:
- Consider all options carefully. (仔细考虑所有选项。)
- I consider him a good friend. (我认为他是好朋友。)
- We should consider her feelings. (我们应该考虑她的感受。)

---
### 368. influence
**音标**: /ˈɪnfluəns/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 影响；改变；势力
**英文释义**: to affect / to have an effect on / power
**词根**: Latin *influere*
**一词多义**:
- The weather influences our mood. (天气影响我们的心情。)
- Music influences people greatly. (音乐对人的影响很大。)
- He has a lot of influence in politics. (他在政界很有影响力。)

---
### 369. effect
**音标**: /ɪˈfekt/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 实现；导致；效果；作用
**英文释义**: to accomplish / to bring about / a result / an impact
**词根**: Latin *effectus*
**一词多义**:
- The new policy effected major changes. (新政策实现了重大变革。)
- The medicine had no effect. (这药没有效果。)
- The effect of the drug was immediate. (药物的作用是立竿见影的。)
- Side effects may occur. (可能会产生副作用。)

---
### 370. happen
**音标**: /ˈhæpən/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 发生；碰巧
**英文释义**: to take place / to chance
**词根**: Old French *happener*
**一词多义**:
- What happened? (发生了什么？)
- I happened to be there. (我碰巧在那里。)
- Accidents will happen. (事故是难免的。)

---
### 371. represent
**音标**: /ˌreprɪˈzent/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 代表；表示；描绘
**英文释义**: to stand for / to express / to depict
**词根**: Latin *repraesentare*
**一词多义**:
- The flag represents our country. (国旗代表我们的国家。)
- This painting represents peace. (这幅画表示和平。)
- He represents our company well. (他很好地代表了我们公司。)

---
### 372. produce
**音标**: /prəˈdjuːs/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 生产；制造；创作
**英文释义**: to make / to manufacture / to create
**词根**: Latin *producere*
**一词多义**:
- The factory produces 1000 cars a day. (这家工厂每天生产1000辆汽车。)
- She produced a wonderful meal. (她做了一顿美餐。)
- The artist produced a masterpiece. (艺术家创作了一幅杰作。)

---
### 373. design
**音标**: /dɪˈzaɪn/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 设计；计划；构思
**英文释义**: to plan / to create / to conceive
**词根**: Latin *designare*
**一词多义**:
- Design a beautiful house. (设计一栋漂亮的房子。)
- The plan was well designed. (计划设计得很好。)
- The design of the garden is lovely. (花园的设计很可爱。)

---
### 374. argue

**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 争论；辩论；主张
**英文释义**: to debate / to discuss / to contend
**词根**: Latin *arguere*
**一词多义**:
- They argued about politics. (他们争论政治问题。)
- I argue that this is wrong. (我认为这是错误的。)
- The evidence argues for his innocence. (证据表明他是无辜的。)

---
### 375. demand
**音标**: /dɪˈmɑːnd/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 要求；需要；查询
**英文释义**: to ask firmly / to need / an inquiry
**词根**: Latin *demandare*
**一词多义**:
- I demand an explanation. (我要求一个解释。)
- The work demands patience. (这项工作需要耐心。)
- There is a high demand for teachers. (对教师的需求很大。)

---
### 376. recognize
**音标**: /ˈrekəɡnaɪz/
**词性**: v.

**英文释义**: to identify / to acknowledge / to realize
**词根**: Latin *recognoscere*
**一词多义**:
- I recognized her voice. (我认出了她的声音。)
- We recognize his achievements. (我们承认他的成就。)
- She recognized the importance of education. (她认识到教育的重要性。)

---
### 377. compare
**音标**: /kəmˈpeə/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 比较；相比；比喻

**词根**: Latin *comparare*

- Compare your answers with mine. (把你的答案和我的比较一下。)
- Life is compared to a journey. (人生被比作旅程。)
- Compared to Beijing, Shanghai is bigger. (和北京相比，上海更大。)

### 378. concern
**音标**: /kənˈsɜːn/
**词性**: v./n.

**词根**: Latin *concernere*
**一词多义**:
- This issue concerns us all. (这个问题关系到我们所有人。)
- I'm concerned about your health. (我担心你的健康。)
- Show concern for others. (对他人表示关心。)

---
### 379. base
**音标**: /beɪs/
**词性**: v./n.

- The base of the mountain is steep. (山脚很陡。)
- The military base was secret. (军事基地是保密的。)

---
### 380. provide
**音标**: /prəˈvaɪd/

**英文释义**: to supply / to equip / to stipulate

- They provided us with food. (他们为我们提供食物。)
- The law provides for equal rights. (法律规定了平等权利。)

### 381. result

**英文释义**: an outcome / to lead to

- Hard work results in success. (努力工作导致成功。)
- Nothing resulted from the meeting. (会议没有任何成果。)

---
### 382. expect
**音标**: /ɪkˈspekt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 期待；预料；期望
**英文释义**: to look forward to / to anticipate / to hope
**词根**: Latin *exspectare*

- Don't expect too much. (不要期望太高。)

---
### 383. create
**音标**: /kriˈeɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 创造；创作；引起
**英文释义**: to bring into existence / to produce / to cause

### 384. maintain

- Maintain your speed. (保持你的速度。)
- He maintains that he is right. (他坚持认为他是正确的。)
- They maintain a family. (他们供养一个家庭。)

---
### 385. treat
**音标**: /triːt/
**词性**: v./n.

**一词多义**:
- Treat others with respect. (尊重他人。)
- The doctor treats patients. (医生治疗病人。)
- Let me treat you to dinner. (让我请你吃饭。)

---
### 386. believe
**音标**: /bɪˈliːv/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 相信；认为；信仰
**英文释义**: to accept as true / to think / to have faith
**词根**: Old English *beleafan*
**一词多义**:
- I believe you. (我相信你。)
- She believes in God. (她信仰上帝。)
- Believe it or not. (信不信由你。)

---
### 387. find
**音标**: /faɪnd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 找到；发现；感到；裁决
**英文释义**: to locate / to discover / to feel / to determine
**词根**: Old English *findan*
**一词多义**:
- Find the answer quickly. (快速找到答案。)
- I found a good restaurant. (我发现了一家好餐厅。)
- I find it difficult. (我觉得这很难。)
- The court found him guilty. (法庭判定他有罪。)

---
### 388. sense
**音标**: /sens/
**词性**: v./n.

**英文释义**: to perceive / a feeling / meaning / faculty
**词根**: Latin *sensus*
**一词多义**:
- I sense something wrong. (我感觉到有什么不对。)
- Common sense tells us the truth. (常识告诉我们真相。)
- The word has many senses. (这个词有很多含义。)
- The five senses are important. (五种感官很重要。)

---
### 389. view
**音标**: /vjuː/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 看待；观看；视野；观点
**英文释义**: to consider / to watch / a sight / an opinion
**词根**: Latin *videre*
**一词多义**:
- How do you view the situation? (你怎么看这个情况？)
- We viewed the sunset from the hill. (我们从山上观看日落。)
- The view from here is beautiful. (从这里看到的景色很美。)
- In my view, you're wrong. (在我看来，你错了。)

---
### 390. gain

**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 获得；增加；获利；增益
**英文释义**: to obtain / to increase / profit / benefit

**一词多义**:
- Gain knowledge through reading. (通过阅读获得知识。)
- The company gained profit. (公司获利了。)
- His speech gained applause. (他的演讲获得了掌声。)
- No pain, no gain. (不劳无获。)

### 391. avoid

### 392. apply

### 393. involve

---
### 394. expect
**音标**: /ɪkˈspekt/

---
### 395. remember

---
### 396. prefer

### 397. argue

**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 争论；辩论；认为
**英文释义**: to debate / to contend / to maintain
**词根**: Latin *arguere*
**一词多义**:

---
### 398. indicate

---
### 399. suggest

**英文释义**: to propose / to imply / to indicate
**词根**: Latin *suggerere*
**一词多义**:

---
### 400. require

**英文释义**: to need / to demand / to make necessary
**词根**: Latin *requirere*

- You are required to attend. (你必须参加。)
- The situation requires immediate action. (情况需要立即采取行动。)

---
### 401. develop

**中文释义**: 发展；开发；冲洗；患
**英文释义**: to grow / to create / to process / to contract
**词根**: Old French *desveloper*
**一词多义**:
- Develop new skills. (发展新技能。)
- They developed a new product. (他们开发了一个新产品。)
- He developed a cold. (他感冒了。)

---
### 402. create

**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 创造；创作；引起
**英文释义**: to bring into being / to produce / to cause
**词根**: Latin *creare*
**一词多义**:
- Create a better future. (创造更美好的未来。)
- She created a masterpiece. (她创作了一幅杰作。)
- The problem created chaos. (这个问题引起了混乱。)

---
### 403. produce
**音标**: /prəˈdjuːs/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 生产；制造；创作；出示
**英文释义**: to make / to manufacture / to compose / to show
**词根**: Latin *producere*
**一词多义**:
- The factory produces cars. (工厂生产汽车。)
- She produced evidence. (她出示了证据。)
- The play was produced by a famous director. (这出戏是由一位著名导演创作的。)

---
### 404. describe
**音标**: /dɪˈskraɪb/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 描述；形容；描绘
**英文释义**: to depict / to characterize / to portray
**词根**: Latin *describere*
**一词多义**:
- Describe what you see. (描述你看到的。)
- The book describes her life. (这本书描述了她的一生。)
- Can you describe him? (你能形容一下他吗？)

---
### 405. explain
**音标**: /ɪkˈspleɪn/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 解释；说明；辩解
**英文释义**: to make clear / to elucidate / to justify
**词根**: Latin *explanare*
**一词多义**:
- Explain this problem. (解释这个问题。)
- Please explain your answer. (请说明你的答案。)
- Can you explain why you're late? (你能解释一下为什么迟到吗？)

---
### 406. matter
**音标**: /ˈmætə/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 要紧；问题；物质
**英文释义**: to be important / an issue / substance
**词根**: Old French *matere*
**一词多义**:
- It doesn't matter. (没关系。)
- What's the matter? (怎么了？)
- Matter exists in different forms. (物质以不同形式存在。)

---
### 407. increase
**音标**: /ɪnˈkriːs/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 增加；增长；提高
**英文释义**: to make greater / to grow / to raise
**词根**: Latin *increscere*
**一词多义**:
- Increase the speed. (提高速度。)
- The population is increasing. (人口在增长。)
- There was an increase in sales. (销售额增加了。)

---
### 408. continue
**音标**: /kənˈtɪnjuː/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 继续；持续；延伸
**英文释义**: to go on / to last / to extend
**词根**: Latin *continuare*
**一词多义**:
- Continue your work. (继续你的工作。)
- The road continues for miles. (这条路延伸数英里。)
- The rain continued all day. (雨下了一整天。)

---
### 409. provide
**音标**: /prəˈvaɪd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 提供；装备；规定
**英文释义**: to supply / to equip / to stipulate
**词根**: Latin *providere*
**一词多义**:
- Provide food and shelter. (提供食物和住所。)
- They provided us with help. (他们为我们提供了帮助。)
- The contract provides for sick leave. (合同规定了病假条款。)

---
### 410. describe
**音标**: /dɪˈskraɪb/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 描述；形容；说明
**英文释义**: to depict / to characterize / to account for
**词根**: Latin *describere*
**一词多义**:
- Describe your experience. (描述你的经历。)
- It's hard to describe. (这很难描述。)
- Words cannot describe the beauty. (言语无法形容这美。)

---
### 411. include
**音标**: /ɪnˈkluːd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 包括；包含；算入
**英文释义**: to contain / to comprise / to count in
**词根**: Latin *includere*
**一词多义**:
- The price includes tax. (价格含税。)
- Your duties include cleaning. (你的职责包括打扫。)
- Please include me in the list. (请把我算在名单里。)

---
### 412. study
**音标**: /ˈstʌdi/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 学习；研究；调查；书房
**英文释义**: to learn / to investigate / a room for study
**词根**: Latin *studium*
**一词多义**:
- Study hard. (努力学习。)
- The study of language is fascinating. (语言研究很吸引人。)
- We conducted a study. (我们进行了一项研究。)
- He has a small study. (他有一间小书房。)

---
### 413. form
**音标**: /fɔːm/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 形成；构成；形式；形状
**英文释义**: to shape / to constitute / a shape / appearance
**词根**: Latin *forma*
**一词多义**:
- Form good habits. (养成好习惯。)
- Water forms ice. (水形成冰。)
- There are different forms of energy. (有不同形式的能量。)
- The sculpture is in the form of a bird. (雕塑是鸟的形状。)

---
### 414. relate
**音标**: /rɪˈleɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 联系；讲述；涉及
**英文释义**: to connect / to narrate / to have reference
**词根**: Latin *relatus*
**一词多义**:
- Relate the events clearly. (清楚地讲述事件。)
- The news relates to our plan. (这条新闻与我们的计划有关。)
- I can relate to that feeling. (我能理解那种感觉。)

---
### 415. decide
**音标**: /dɪˈsaɪd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 决定；判决；解决
**英文释义**: to make a choice / to judge / to resolve
**词根**: Latin *decidere*
**一词多义**:
- Decide what you want. (决定你想要什么。)
- The judge decided the case. (法官判决了这个案件。)
- We decided to go. (我们决定去。)

---
### 416. appear
**音标**: /əˈpɪə/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 出现；显得；出版
**英文释义**: to come into view / to seem / to be published
**词根**: Latin *apparere*
**一词多义**:
- A solution appeared. (出现了一个解决方案。)
- She appears happy. (她看起来很开心。)
- The book appears next week. (书下周出版。)

---
### 417. support
**音标**: /səˈpɔːt/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 支持；支撑；赞助
**英文释义**: to hold up / to back / to sponsor
**词根**: Latin *supportare*
**一词多义**:
- Support your local team. (支持你当地的队伍。)
- The chair supports the weight. (椅子承受重量。)
- Thank you for your support. (谢谢你的支持。)

---
### 418. produce
**音标**: /prəˈdjuːs/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 生产；创作；出示
**英文释义**: to make / to create / to bring forward
**词根**: Latin *producere*
**一词多义**:
- Produce more goods. (生产更多商品。)
- She produced a report. (她出示了一份报告。)
- The artist produces beautiful work. (艺术家创作美丽的作品。)

---
### 419. base
**音标**: /beɪs/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 基于；基础；基地
**英文释义**: to establish / foundation / center
**词根**: Greek *basis*
**一词多义**:
- Base your decision on facts. (让你的决定基于事实。)
- The base of the tower is firm. (塔的地基很牢固。)
- It's a military base. (那是军事基地。)

---
### 420. remain
**音标**: /rɪˈmeɪn/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 保持；仍然是；剩下
**英文释义**: to stay / to continue to be / to be left
**词根**: Latin *manere*
**一词多义**:
- Remain calm. (保持冷静。)
- The result remains the same. (结果仍然相同。)
- A few items remain. (还剩几件物品。)

## Day 15
### 421. scrutinize
**音标**: /ˈskruːtɪnaɪz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 仔细检查；详细审查
**英文释义**: to examine in detail / to inspect thoroughly
**词根**: Latin *scrutinium*
**一词多义**:
- The auditor scrutinized the accounts. (审计员仔细检查了账目。)
- She scrutinized his face for any sign of guilt. (她仔细审视他的脸寻找愧疚的迹象。)
- Customs officers scrutinize luggage. (海关官员仔细检查行李。)

---
### 422. hypothesis
**音标**: /haɪˈpɒθɪsɪs/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 假设；假说
**英文释义**: a proposed explanation / an assumption
**词根**: Greek *hypothesis*
**一词多义**:
- The scientist tested her hypothesis. (科学家验证了她的假设。)
- This hypothesis needs proof. (这个假说需要证明。)
- His hypothesis was correct. (他的假设是正确的。)

---
### 423. paradigm
**音标**: /ˈpærədaɪm/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 范式；典范；样板
**英文释义**: a typical example / a model / a framework
**词根**: Greek *paradeigma*
**一词多义**:
- This discovery created a new paradigm. (这个发现创造了一个新范式。)
- He is a paradigm of virtue. (他是美德的典范。)
- The paradigm shift changed the field. (范式转变改变了这个领域。)

---
### 424. synthesize
**音标**: /ˈsɪnθɪsaɪz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 综合；合成
**英文释义**: to combine elements / to create by synthesis
**词根**: Greek *synthesis*
**一词多义**:
- The chemist synthesized a new compound. (化学家合成了一种新化合物。)
- We need to synthesize information from various sources. (我们需要综合各种来源的信息。)
- The report synthesizes data from multiple studies. (报告综合了多项研究的数据。)

---
### 425. coherent
**音标**: /kəʊˈhɪərənt/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 连贯的；一致的；有条理的
**英文释义**: logical and consistent / clear
**词根**: Latin *cohaerere*
**一词多义**:
- She gave a coherent explanation. (她给出了一个连贯的解释。)
- The argument was not coherent. (论点不一致。)
- He struggled to form coherent sentences. (他难以形成有条理的话语。)

---
### 426. empirical
**音标**: /ɪmˈpɪrɪkəl/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 经验的；实证的
**英文释义**: based on observation / experimental
**词根**: Greek *empeirikos*
**一词多义**:
- The study provides empirical evidence. (这项研究提供了经验证据。)
- Empirical research is important. (实证研究很重要。)
- We need empirical data to support this. (我们需要经验数据来支持这一点。)

---
### 427. phenomenon
**音标**: /fɪˈnɒmɪnən/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 现象；奇迹
**英文释义**: an observed fact / a remarkable event
**词根**: Greek *phainomenon*
**一词多义**:
- The phenomenon is rare. (这种现象很罕见。)
- Climate change is a global phenomenon. (气候变化是全球性现象。)
- They studied the phenomenon closely. (他们仔细研究了这一现象。)

---
### 428. inherent
**音标**: /ɪnˈhɪərənt/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 固有的；内在的；本质的
**英文释义**: existing as a natural quality / intrinsic
**词根**: Latin *inhaerere*
**一词多义**:
- There are inherent risks in this investment. (这项投资有固有风险。)
- The inherent beauty of nature. (自然的内在美。)
- This is an inherent weakness. (这是一个固有的弱点。)

---
### 429. ambiguous
**音标**: /æmˈbɪɡjuəs/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 模糊不清的；引起歧义的
**英文释义**: unclear / open to multiple interpretations
**词根**: Latin *ambiguus*
**一词多义**:
- The wording is ambiguous. (措辞模糊不清。)
- Her response was ambiguous. (她的回答含糊不清。)
- We must avoid ambiguous statements. (我们必须避免含糊不清的陈述。)

---
### 430. systematic
**音标**: /ˌsɪstəˈmætɪk/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 系统的；有条理的
**英文释义**: done according to a system / methodical
**词根**: Greek *systema*
**一词多义**:
- We need a systematic approach. (我们需要系统的方法。)
- She has a systematic mind. (她有有条理的头脑。)
- The problem requires systematic analysis. (这个问题需要系统的分析。)

---
### 431. exemplify
**音标**: /ɪɡˈzemplɪfaɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 作为…的典型；举例说明
**英文释义**: to be a typical example / to illustrate
**词根**: Latin *exemplum*
**一词多义**:
- She exemplifies patience. (她是耐心的典型。)
- The chart exemplifies our point. (图表举例说明了我们的观点。)
- This case exemplifies the problem. (这个案例说明了这个问题。)

---
### 432. comprehensive
**音标**: /ˌkɒmprɪˈhensɪv/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 全面的；综合的；理解的
**英文释义**: including all aspects / thorough
**词根**: Latin *comprehendere*
**一词多义**:
- We need a comprehensive review. (我们需要全面审查。)
- The book provides comprehensive coverage. (这本书有全面的覆盖。)
- She has a comprehensive understanding. (她有全面的理解。)

---
### 433. preliminary
**音标**: /prɪˈlɪmɪnəri/
**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 初步的；预备的；初步工作
**英文释义**: coming before the main / initial
**词根**: Latin *praeliminaris*
**一词多义**:
- The preliminary results are promising. (初步结果很有希望。)
- We need preliminary data. (我们需要初步数据。)
- They conducted a preliminary study. (他们进行了初步研究。)

---
### 434. redundant
**音标**: /rɪˈdʌndənt/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 多余的；冗余的；被裁员的
**英文释义**: not needed / excessive / dismissed from work
**词根**: Latin *redundare*
**一词多义**:
- The report contains redundant information. (报告包含多余信息。)
- The system has redundant backups. (系统有冗余备份。)
- Many workers were made redundant. (许多工人被裁员了。)

---
### 435. autonomous
**音标**: /ɔːˈtɒnəməs/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 自治的；自主的；自发的
**英文释义**: self-governing / independent / self-directed
**词根**: Greek *autonomos*
**一词多义**:
- The region is autonomous. (该地区是自治的。)
- She runs an autonomous organization. (她管理一个自主的组织。)
- The car can drive in autonomous mode. (汽车可以自动驾驶。)

---
### 436. hypothesis
**音标**: /haɪˈpɒθɪsɪs/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 假设；假说；前提
**英文释义**: a tentative explanation / an assumption
**词根**: Greek *hypothesis*
**一词多义**:
- Form a hypothesis. (形成一个假设。)
- Test the hypothesis. (检验这个假设。)
- Based on this hypothesis, we proceed. (基于这个前提我们继续进行。)

---
### 437. qualitative
**音标**: /ˈkwɒlɪtətɪv/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 定性的；质量的；品质的
**英文释义**: relating to quality / not measurable
**词根**: Latin *qualitas*
**一词多义**:
- Qualitative analysis is important. (定性分析很重要。)
- We need qualitative improvements. (我们需要质量上的改进。)
- The qualitative study revealed insights. (定性研究揭示了见解。)

---
### 438. quantitative
**音标**: /ˈkwɒntɪtətɪv/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 定量的；数量的
**英文释义**: relating to quantity / measurable
**词根**: Latin *quantitas*
**一词多义**:
- Quantitative research is essential. (定量研究至关重要。)
- We need quantitative data. (我们需要定量数据。)
- There were quantitative changes. (发生了数量上的变化。)

---
### 439. prevalent
**音标**: /ˈprevələnt/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 流行的；普遍的；广泛存在的
**英文释义**: widespread / commonly occurring
**词根**: Latin *praevalere*
**一词多义**:
- The disease is prevalent in this area. (这种疾病在这个地区很流行。)
- Smartphones are prevalent nowadays. (智能手机如今很普遍。)
- This view is prevalent among students. (这种观点在学生中很普遍。)

---
### 440. viable
**音标**: /ˈvaɪəbl/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 可行的；能生存的；可实施的
**英文释义**: practicable / capable of surviving / feasible
**词根**: Latin *vita*
**一词多义**:
- Is this a viable solution? (这是一个可行的解决方案吗？)
- The business must be viable. (企业必须能够生存。)
- We need a viable alternative. (我们需要一个可行的替代方案。)

---
### 441. innovative
**音标**: /ˈɪnəveɪtɪv/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 创新的；革新的；有创新精神的
**英文释义**: introducing new ideas / creative
**词根**: Latin *innovare*
**一词多义**:
- We need innovative solutions. (我们需要创新解决方案。)
- She is an innovative thinker. (她是一个有创新精神的思想者。)
- The company is known for innovative products. (这家公司以创新产品著称。)

---
### 442. substantiate
**音标**: /səbˈstænʃɪeɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 证实；证明；实体化
**英文释义**: to prove the truth of / to give substance to
**词根**: Latin *substantia*
**一词多义**:
- Can you substantiate your claim? (你能证实你的说法吗？)
- The evidence substantiates the theory. (证据证实了这个理论。)
- We need to substantiate our findings. (我们需要证实我们的发现。)

---
### 443. deteriorate
**音标**: /dɪˈtɪərɪəreɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 恶化；变坏；退化
**英文释义**: to become worse / to decline
**词根**: Latin *deterior*
**一词多义**:
- His health deteriorated quickly. (他的健康状况迅速恶化。)
- The situation is deteriorating. (情况正在恶化。)
- Relations between the countries deteriorated. (两国关系恶化了。)

---
### 444. ameliorate
**音标**: /əˈmiːlɪəreɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 改善；改进；变得更好
**英文释义**: to make better / to improve
**词根**: Latin *ameliorare*
**一词多义**:
- We must ameliorate the situation. (我们必须改善情况。)
- The policy ameliorated living conditions. (政策改善了生活条件。)
- Technology can ameliorate many problems. (科技能改善许多问题。)

---
### 445. mitigate
**音标**: /ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 减轻；缓和；使平息
**英文释义**: to make less severe / to alleviate
**词根**: Latin *mitigare*
**一词多义**:
- We must mitigate the risks. (我们必须减轻风险。)
- The drug mitigates pain. (这种药能减轻疼痛。)
- Measures to mitigate climate change. (应对气候变化的措施。)

---
### 446. exacerbate
**音标**: /ɪɡˈzæsəbeɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 使恶化；使加剧；使加重
**英文释义**: to make worse / to aggravate
**词根**: Latin *exacerbare*
**一词多义**:
- The drought exacerbated the food shortage. (干旱加剧了粮食短缺。)
- His comments exacerbated the tension. (他的话加剧了紧张局势。)
- Don't exacerbate the problem. (不要使问题恶化。)

---
### 447. circumvent
**音标**: /ˌsɜːkəmˈvent/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 绕过；规避；回避
**英文释义**: to go around / to avoid / to bypass
**词根**: Latin *circumvenire*
**一词多义**:
- They circumvented the rules. (他们规避了规则。)
- We found a way to circumvent the obstacle. (我们找到了绕过障碍的方法。)
- How did they circumvent the system? (他们是如何绕过系统的？)

---
### 448. expedite
**音标**: /ˈekspɪdaɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 加快；加速；促进
**英文释义**: to speed up / to facilitate
**词根**: Latin *expedire*
**一词多义**:
- We need to expedite the process. (我们需要加快进程。)
- The new system will expedite delivery. (新系统将加快交付。)
- Can you expedite this order? (你能加快处理这个订单吗？)

---
### 449. facilitate
**音标**: /fəˈsɪlɪteɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 促进；使便利；帮助
**英文释义**: to make easy / to help / to enable
**词根**: Latin *facilis*
**一词多义**:
- Technology facilitates communication. (科技促进交流。)
- The guide facilitated our tour. (向导方便了我们的旅行。)
- A secretary facilitates daily operations. (秘书促进日常工作。)

---
### 450. inhibit
**音标**: /ɪnˈhɪbɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 抑制；禁止；阻止
**英文释义**: to restrain / to prohibit / to prevent
**词根**: Latin *inhibere*
**一词多义**:
- The drug inhibits growth. (这种药抑制生长。)
- Don't inhibit creativity. (不要抑制创造力。)
- Fear inhibited her response. (恐惧抑制了她的反应。)
