## Day 16
### 451. alleviate
**音标**: /əˈliːvɪeɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 减轻；缓和；缓解
**英文释义**: to make less intense / to relieve
**词根**: Latin *alleviare*
**一词多义**:
- The medicine alleviates pain. (这种药缓解疼痛。)
- We need to alleviate suffering. (我们需要减轻痛苦。)
- The policy alleviated poverty. (政策缓解了贫困。)

---
### 452. comprise
**音标**: /kəmˈpraɪz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 包含；由…组成；构成
**英文释义**: to include / to consist of / to form
**词根**: Latin *comprehendere*
**一词多义**:
- The team comprises experts. (团队由专家组成。)
- Women comprise 50% of the population. (女性占人口的50%。)
- This comprises our total inventory. (这是我们的全部库存。)

---
### 453. constitute
**音标**: /ˈkɒnstɪtjuːt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 构成；设立；任命
**英文释义**: to form / to establish / to appoint
**词根**: Latin *constituere*
**一词多义**:
- These facts constitute proof. (这些事实构成证据。)
- They constituted a new committee. (他们设立了一个新委员会。)
- The act constituted a crime. (该行为构成犯罪。)

---
### 454. encompass
**音标**: /ɪnˈkʌmpəs/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 包含；围绕；包围
**英文释义**: to include / to surround / to encircle
**词根**: Old French *encombrer*
**一词多义**:
- The study encompasses all ages. (这项研究涵盖所有年龄。)
- The city encompasses the old town. (城市包围着老城区。)
- His interests encompass many fields. (他的兴趣涉及很多领域。)

---
### 455. predominant
**音标**: /prɪˈdɒmɪnənt/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 主要的；主导的；突出的
**英文释义**: most noticeable / dominant / main
**词根**: Latin *praedominari*
**一词多义**:
- Blue is the predominant color. (蓝色是主要颜色。)
- The predominant emotion was fear. (主要情绪是恐惧。)
- English is predominant in this region. (英语在这个地区占主导地位。)

---
### 456. prevalent
**音标**: /ˈprevələnt/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 流行的；普遍的；广泛存在的
**英文释义**: common / widespread / prevailing
**词根**: Latin *praevalere*
**一词多义**:
- The disease is prevalent here. (这种疾病在这里很流行。)
- Smartphones are prevalent. (智能手机很普及。)
- This opinion is increasingly prevalent. (这种观点越来越普遍。)

---
### 457. preliminary
**音标**: /prɪˈlɪmɪnəri/
**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 初步的；预备的；初步工作
**英文释义**: initial / preparatory / a preliminary event
**词根**: Latin *praeliminaris*
**一词多义**:
- The preliminary findings are positive. (初步发现是积极的。)
- We need preliminary approval. (我们需要初步批准。)
- After preliminaries, the main event began. (预赛结束后主要活动开始了。)

---
### 458. preliminary
**音标**: /prɪˈlɪmɪnəri/
**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 预备的；初步的；前置工作
**英文释义**: leading up to the main / introductory
**词根**: Latin *praeliminaris*
**一词多义**:
- We held preliminary discussions. (我们进行了初步讨论。)
- The preliminary round determines finalists. (预赛决定决赛选手。)
- There are preliminary requirements. (有初步要求。)

---
### 459. subsequent
**音标**: /ˈsʌbsɪkwənt/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 后来的；随后的；接着发生的
**英文释义**: following / later / coming after
**词根**: Latin *subsequi*
**一词多义**:
- Subsequent events proved me right. (随后的事情证明我是对的。)
- No subsequent problems occurred. (没有出现后续问题。)
- The book was published in 2020 and subsequent editions followed. (这本书于2020年出版随后有后续版本。)

---
### 460. preceding
**音标**: /prɪˈsiːdɪŋ/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 在前的；前述的；先于…的
**英文释义**: coming before / previous
**词根**: Latin *prae* + *cedere*
**一词多义**:
- The preceding chapter discussed this. (前几章讨论过这个。)
- In the preceding years, things changed. (在前几年情况发生了变化。)
- Preceding the meeting, we had lunch. (会议之前我们吃了午饭。)

---
### 461. precedent
**音标**: /ˈpresɪdənt/
**词性**: n./adj.
**中文释义**: 先例；惯例；先前的
**英文释义**: an earlier example / previous
**词根**: Latin *praecedere*
**一词多义**:
- This sets a dangerous precedent. (这开创了一个危险的先例。)
- There is no precedent for this. (此事没有先例。)
- The judge cited precedent. (法官引用了先例。)

---
### 462. precedent
**音标**: /ˈpresɪdənt/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 先例；判例；惯例
**英文释义**: a legal decision / an established practice
**词根**: Latin *praecedere*
**一词多义**:
- This case could set a precedent. (这个案件可以创下先例。)
- Historical precedents support this. (历史先例支持这一点。)
- We must break from precedent. (我们必须打破惯例。)

---
### 463. predecessor
**音标**: /ˈpriːdɪsesə/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 前任；前辈；原有事物
**英文释义**: someone who held a position before / a previous thing
**词根**: Latin *prae* + *decidere*
**一词多义**:
- My predecessor held this job for 20 years. (我的前任做了20年。)
- The new plan is better than its predecessor. (新计划比原来的好。)
- Our products outperform predecessors. (我们的产品超过前辈。)

---
### 464. innovate
**音标**: /ˈɪnəveɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 创新；改革；引入新事物
**英文释义**: to introduce new ideas / to make changes
**词根**: Latin *innovare*
**一词多义**:
- We must innovate to survive. (我们必须创新才能生存。)
- The company innovates constantly. (公司不断创新。)
- They innovated in the field of education. (他们在教育领域进行了创新。)

---
### 465. revolutionize
**音标**: /ˌrevəˈluːʃənaɪz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 彻底改变；使革命化
**英文释义**: to fundamentally change / to transform
**词根**: Latin *revolutio*
**一词多义**:
- The internet revolutionized communication. (互联网彻底改变了通讯。)
- This technology will revolutionize industry. (这项技术将彻底改变工业。)
- They revolutionized the design. (他们彻底改变了设计。)

---
### 466. transform
**音标**: /trænsˈfɔːm/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 改变；改造；转换
**英文释义**: to change completely / to convert
**词根**: Latin *transformare*
**一词多义**:
- The experience transformed me. (这段经历改变了我。)
- We transform raw materials. (我们加工原材料。)
- A caterpillar transforms into a butterfly. (毛毛虫变成蝴蝶。)

---
### 467. convert
**音标**: /kənˈvɜːt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 转换；改变；皈依
**英文释义**: to change / to transform / to bring to a new religion
**词根**: Latin *convertere*
**一词多义**:
- We convert sunlight to energy. (我们把阳光转化为能量。)
- She converted to Buddhism. (她皈依佛教。)
- The building was converted into a school. (这栋建筑被改建成学校。)

---
### 468. modify
**音标**: /ˈmɒdɪfaɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 修改；改变；修饰
**英文释义**: to change partially / to alter / to qualify
**词根**: Latin *modificare*
**一词多义**:
- We need to modify the plan. (我们需要修改计划。)
- The design was modified. (设计被修改了。)
- Adjectives modify nouns. (形容词修饰名词。)

---
### 469. alter
**音标**: /ˈɔːltə/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 改变；改动；阉割
**英文释义**: to change / to modify / to castrate
**词根**: Latin *alterare*
**一词多义**:
- You can alter your plans. (你可以改变计划。)

- The tailor altered the dress. (裁缝改了裙子。)

---
### 470. revise

**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 修订；修改；复习
**英文释义**: to review and correct / to amend / to study again
**词根**: Latin *revisere*
**一词多义**:
- Please revise your essay. (请修改你的文章。)
- We need to revise the policy. (我们需要修改政策。)
- I must revise for exams. (我必须复习备考。)

---
### 471. ameliorate

**词性**: v.

**英文释义**: to make better / to improve
**词根**: Latin *ameliorare*
**一词多义**:
- We must ameliorate working conditions. (我们必须改善工作条件。)
- The medicine ameliorated symptoms. (药物改善了症状。)
- New policies ameliorated poverty. (新政策改善了贫困状况。)

---
### 472. mitigate

**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 减轻；缓和；使平息
**英文释义**: to lessen / to make less severe
**词根**: Latin *mitigare*
**一词多义**:
- We must mitigate environmental damage. (我们必须减轻环境损害。)
- His apology mitigated her anger. (他的道歉缓和了她的愤怒。)
- Trees mitigate the effects of pollution. (树木减轻污染的影响。)

---
### 473. alleviate

**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 减轻；缓和；缓解
**英文释义**: to relieve / to make less intense
**词根**: Latin *alleviare*
**一词多义**:
- The treatment alleviates suffering. (治疗减轻痛苦。)
- The government alleviated the crisis. (政府缓解了危机。)
- Music alleviates stress. (音乐缓解压力。)

---
### 474. diminish

**英文释义**: to reduce / to decrease / to belittle
**词根**: Latin *diminutio*
**一词多义**:
- Time diminishes memory. (时间会降低记忆力。)
- Don't diminish his achievements. (不要贬低他的成就。)
- The medicine diminished the pain. (药物减轻了疼痛。)

---
### 475. degenerate

**英文释义**: to decline / to become worse / degraded
**词根**: Latin *degenerare*
**一词多义**:
- His condition degenerated. (他的状况恶化了。)

- The building is in a degenerated state. (建筑处于破败状态。)

---
### 476. deteriorate

**词性**: v.

**英文释义**: to become progressively worse / to decay
**词根**: Latin *deterior*
**一词多义**:
- The patient's health deteriorated. (病人的健康恶化了。)
- Relations between them deteriorated. (他们之间的关系恶化了。)
- Materials deteriorate over time. (材料会随时间老化。)

---
### 477. exacerbate

**词性**: v.

**英文释义**: to make bad situations worse
**词根**: Latin *exacerbare*
**一词多义**:
- The heat exacerbated the problem. (高温使问题恶化。)

- Don't exacerbate the situation. (不要使情况恶化。)

---
### 478. aggravate

**英文释义**: to make worse / to irritate
**词根**: Latin *gravare*
**一词多义**:
- The drought aggravated the food crisis. (干旱加重了粮食危机。)
- His attitude aggravated her. (他的态度激怒了她。)
- Lack of sleep aggravates stress. (睡眠不足加重压力。)

---
### 479. intensify

**词性**: v.

**英文释义**: to make stronger / to increase
**词根**: Latin *intensus*
**一词多义**:
- The army intensified its attacks. (军队加强了攻击。)
- The debate intensified. (辩论加剧了。)
- We must intensify our efforts. (我们必须加强努力。)

---
### 480. escalate

- The conflict escalated. (冲突升级了。)

- The situation escalated out of control. (局势升级到失控。)

### 482. emanate

### 483. impose

### 484. permeate

### 485. penetrate

### 486. pervade

### 487. propagate

### 488. disseminate

### 489. diffuse

---
### 490. disperse

### 491. scatter

### 492. suppress

### 493. repress

**中文释义**: 镇压；抑制；约束
**英文释义**: to quell / to restrain / to hold back
**词根**: Latin *reprimere*
**一词多义**:
- The regime repressed dissent. (该政权镇压异议。)
- He repressed his emotions. (他抑制了自己的情绪。)
- The memory was repressed. (那段记忆被压抑了。)

---
### 494. curb
**音标**: /kɜːb/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 控制；约束；抑制；路缘
**英文释义**: to restrain / to check / the edge of a sidewalk
**词根**: Latin *curvare*
**一词多义**:
- We must curb corruption. (我们必须遏制腐败。)
- Curbs on spending are needed. (需要限制开支。)
- He sat on the curb. (他坐在路缘上。)

---
### 495. restrain
**音标**: /rɪˈstreɪn/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 阻止；抑制；控制
**英文释义**: to hold back / to prevent / to restrict
**词根**: Latin *restringere*
**一词多义**:
- Restrain the dog. (把狗拴住。)
- I couldn't restrain my anger. (我抑制不住愤怒。)
- The police restrained him. (警察阻止了他。)

---
### 496. constrain
**音标**: /kənˈstreɪn/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 强迫；限制；约束
**英文释义**: to force / to limit / to restrict
**词根**: Latin *constringere*
**一词多义**:
- Budget constraints limit our options. (预算限制限制了我们的选择。)
- She felt constrained by the rules. (她感觉被规则束缚了。)
- We are constrained to act. (我们被迫行动。)

---
### 497. inhibit
**音标**: /ɪnˈhɪbɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 抑制；禁止；阻止
**英文释义**: to restrain / to prohibit / to hold back
**词根**: Latin *inhibere*
**一词多义**:
- The drug inhibits enzyme activity. (这种药抑制酶活性。)
- Shyness inhibited her from speaking. (害羞使她不敢说话。)
- Don't inhibit natural development. (不要抑制自然发展。)

---
### 498. prohibit
**音标**: /prəˈhɪbɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 禁止；阻止；使不可能
**英文释义**: to forbid / to prevent / to make impossible
**词根**: Latin *prohibere*
**一词多义**:
- Smoking is prohibited. (禁止吸烟。)
- The rules prohibit using phones. (规则禁止使用手机。)
- High costs prohibit expansion. (高成本阻止了扩张。)

---
### 499. forbid
**音标**: /fəˈbɪd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 禁止；阻止；不允许
**英文释义**: to ban / to prevent / to disallow
**词根**: Old English *forbeodan*
**一词多义**:
- The law forbids discrimination. (法律禁止歧视。)
- Her health forbids travel. (她的健康状况不允许旅行。)
- I forbid you to go. (我不允许你去。)

---
### 500. sanction
**音标**: /ˈsæŋkʃən/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 批准；制裁；认可
**英文释义**: to authorize / to penalize / official permission
**词根**: Latin *sanctio*
**一词多义**:
- The UN sanctioned the action. (联合国批准了这项行动。)
- Economic sanctions were imposed. (实施了经济制裁。)
- We need official sanction to proceed. (我们需要正式批准才能继续。)

---
### 501. authorize
**音标**: /ˈɔːθəraɪz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 授权；批准；委托
**英文释义**: to give permission / to empower / to approve
**词根**: Latin *auctor*
**一词多义**:
- I am authorized to sign contracts. (我被授权签合同。)
- The president authorized the spending. (总统批准了这项开支。)
- You need authorized access. (你需要授权才能访问。)

---
### 502. legitimate
**音标**: /lɪˈdʒɪtɪmət/
**词性**: adj./v.
**中文释义**: 合法的；合理的；使合法
**英文释义**: lawful / reasonable / to make legal
**词根**: Latin *lex*
**一词多义**:
- That's a legitimate question. (这是合理的问题。)
- The government is legitimate. (政府是合法的。)
- They legitimized the process. (他们使这个过程合法化。)

---
### 503. valid
**音标**: /ˈvælɪd/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 有效的；正当的；有根据的
**英文释义**: having legal force / sound / well-founded
**词根**: Latin *validus*
**一词多义**:
- Your passport is valid. (你的护照有效。)
- That's a valid point. (那是正当的观点。)
- We need valid reasons. (我们需要有正当理由。)

---
### 504. invalid
**音标**: /ɪnˈvælɪd/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 无效的；无根据的；残疾的
**英文释义**: not valid / unfounded / disabled
**词根**: Latin *invalidus*
**一词多义**:
- The contract is invalid. (合同无效。)
- His argument is invalid. (他的论点没有根据。)
- She cares for her invalid mother. (她照顾残疾的母亲。)

---
### 505. warrant
**音标**: /ˈwɒrənt/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 保证；担保；授权令；正当理由
**英文释义**: to guarantee / to justify / a written authorization / justification
**词根**: Old French *warant*
**一词多义**:
- I warrant the quality. (我保证质量。)
- You have no warrant for this action. (你没有采取这一行动的正当理由。)
- A search warrant was issued. (签发了搜查令。)

---
### 506. certify
**音标**: /ˈsɜːtɪfaɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 证明；发证书；证实
**英文释义**: to confirm / to issue a certificate / to attest
**词根**: Latin *certus*
**一词多义**:
- I certify that this is true. (我证明这是真的。)
- The doctor certified him as fit. (医生证明他身体合格。)
- She is a certified public accountant. (她是一名注册会计师。)

---
### 507. verify
**音标**: /ˈverɪfaɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 核实；证明；确认
**英文释义**: to confirm the truth of / to check
**词根**: Latin *verus*
**一词多义**:
- Please verify your identity. (请核实您的身份。)
- The information verified our suspicions. (信息证实了我们的怀疑。)
- Can you verify this statement? (你能证实这个声明吗？)

---
### 508. validate
**音标**: /ˈvælɪdeɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 验证；使有效；确认
**英文释义**: to confirm validity / to make legally valid / to confirm
**词根**: Latin *validus*
**一词多义**:
- We must validate the results. (我们必须验证结果。)
- The signature needs to be validated. (签名需要确认有效。)
- Your feelings are valid. (你的感受是合理的。)

---
### 509. confirm
**音标**: /kənˈfɜːm/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 确认；证实；批准
**英文释义**: to verify / to prove true / to approve
**词根**: Latin *confirmare*
**一词多义**:
- Please confirm your attendance. (请确认您是否出席。)
- The test confirmed the diagnosis. (测试证实了诊断。)
- The president confirmed the appointment. (总统批准了任命。)

---
### 510. affirm
**音标**: /əˈfɜːm/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 肯定；确认；断言
**英文释义**: to state positively / to confirm / to assert
**词根**: Latin *affirmare*
**一词多义**:
- I affirm my commitment. (我确认我的承诺。)
- The witness affirmed the truth. (证人肯定了真相。)
- The court affirmed the judgment. (法院维持了原判。)

## Day 18
### 511. assert
**音标**: /əˈsɜːt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 断言；主张；维护
**英文释义**: to declare / to maintain / to insist on
**词根**: Latin *asserere*
**一词多义**:
- He asserted his innocence. (他坚称自己无罪。)
- We assert our rights. (我们维护自己的权利。)
- The study asserts that this is true. (研究断言这是真的。)

---
### 512. allege
**音标**: /əˈledʒ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 断言；宣称；指控
**英文释义**: to claim / to assert without proof
**词根**: Latin *allegare*
**一词多义**:
- The newspaper alleges corruption. (报纸指控腐败。)
- She alleges that he lied. (她声称他撒谎了。)
- He is alleged to have stolen money. (他被指控偷钱。)

---
### 513. claim
**音标**: /kleɪm/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 声称；认领；索赔；主张
**英文释义**: to assert / to demand / to say / a demand
**词根**: Latin *clamare*
**一词多义**:
- He claims he is innocent. (他声称自己是无辜的。)
- No one claimed the bag. (没有人认领这个包。)
- They made a claim on the insurance. (他们提出了保险索赔。)

---
### 514. declare
**音标**: /dɪˈkleə/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 宣布；声明；申报
**英文释义**: to announce formally / to state / to report
**词根**: Latin *declarare*
**一词多义**:
- War was declared. (战争爆发了。)
- I declare the meeting open. (我宣布会议开始。)
- You must declare goods at customs. (你必须在海关申报物品。)

---
### 515. proclaim
**音标**: /prəˈkleɪm/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 宣布；公布；表明
**英文释义**: to announce officially / to reveal / to praise
**词根**: Latin *proclamare*
**一词多义**:
- The president proclaimed a holiday. (总统宣布放假。)
- Her face proclaimed her joy. (她的脸表明了她的快乐。)
- They proclaimed him a hero. (他们称他为英雄。)

---
### 516. pronounce
**音标**: /prəˈnaʊns/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 发音；宣布；宣告
**英文释义**: to say / to articulate / to declare
**词根**: Latin *pronuntiare*
**一词多义**:
- How do you pronounce this word? (这个词怎么发音？)
- The doctor pronounced him dead. (医生宣布他死亡。)
- The judge pronounced sentence. (法官宣判了。)

---
### 517. articulate
**音标**: /ɑːˈtɪkjʊleɪt/
**词性**: v./adj.
**中文释义**: 清晰地表达；关节连接的
**英文释义**: to speak clearly / jointed / expressing well
**词根**: Latin *articulus*
**一词多义**:
- She articulated her concerns. (她清楚地表达了她的担忧。)
- The robot has articulated arms. (机器人有关节臂。)
- He is very articulate. (他表达很清楚。)

---
### 518. convey
**音标**: /kənˈveɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 传达；表达；运输
**英文释义**: to communicate / to express / to transport
**词根**: Latin *convehere*
**一词多义**:
- Colors convey emotions. (颜色传达情感。)
- Please convey my regards. (请转达我的问候。)
- Trucks convey goods. (卡车运输货物。)

---
### 519. communicate
**音标**: /kəˈmjuːnɪkeɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 交流；传达；传染
**英文释义**: to share / to convey / to transmit
**词根**: Latin *communicare*
**一词多义**:
- We communicate daily. (我们每天交流。)
- The news was communicated to all. (消息传达给了所有人。)
- The disease can communicate. (这种病会传染。)

---
### 520. transmit
**音标**: /trænzˈmɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 传输；传达；传染
**英文释义**: to send / to pass on / to broadcast
**词根**: Latin *transmittere*
**一词多义**:
- The station transmits news. (电台传输新闻。)
- Parents transmit genes to children. (父母把基因传给孩子。)
- The disease is transmitted by mosquitoes. (这种病通过蚊子传播。)

---
### 521. disclose
**音标**: /dɪsˈkləʊz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 揭露；披露；公开
**英文释义**: to reveal / to make known
**词根**: Old French *desclore*
**一词多义**:
- The report disclosed fraud. (报告揭露了欺诈行为。)
- He refused to disclose his source. (他拒绝透露消息来源。)
- She disclosed her plans. (她透露了她的计划。)

---
### 522. reveal
**音标**: /rɪˈviːl/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 揭示；揭露；显示
**英文释义**: to make known / to show / to uncover
**词根**: Latin *revelare*
**一词多义**:
- The evidence revealed the truth. (证据揭示了真相。)
- She revealed her identity. (她透露了自己的身份。)
- The sky revealed the stars. (天空显现出星星。)

---
### 523. expose
**音标**: /ɪkˈspəʊz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 暴露；揭露；使曝光
**英文释义**: to uncover / to reveal / to subject to
**词根**: Latin *exponere*
**一词多义**:
- Don't expose your skin to the sun. (不要让皮肤暴露在阳光下。)
- The scandal exposed corruption. (丑闻揭露了腐败。)
- He was exposed to danger. (他暴露在危险中。)

---
### 524. uncover
**音标**: /ʌnˈkʌvə/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 揭露；发现；揭开盖子
**英文释义**: to reveal / to discover / to remove a cover
**词根**: Old English *uncoverian*
**一词多义**:
- We uncovered a conspiracy. (我们揭露了一个阴谋。)
- They uncovered the ancient ruins. (他们发现了古代遗址。)
- Please uncover the dish. (请揭开盖子。)

---
### 525. divulge
**音标**: /daɪˈvʌldʒ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 透露；泄露；公开
**英文释义**: to make known / to reveal secrets
**词根**: Latin *divulgare*
**一词多义**:
- He refused to divulge his sources. (他拒绝透露消息来源。)
- The company divulged its plans. (公司透露了计划。)
- It's a secret; don't divulge it. (这是秘密不要泄露。)

---
### 526. manifest
**音标**: /ˈmænɪfest/
**词性**: v./adj.
**中文释义**: 表明；显示；明显的
**英文释义**: to show / to display / obvious / clear
**词根**: Latin *manifestus*
**一词多义**:
- Symptoms manifest themselves. (症状会表现出来。)
- His talent manifested early. (他的天赋很早就显现出来了。)
- There is manifest injustice. (存在明显的injustice。)

---
### 527. demonstrate
**音标**: /ˈdemənstreɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 演示；证明；示威；表现
**英文释义**: to show / to prove / to protest / to exhibit
**词根**: Latin *demonstrare*
**一词多义**:
- The teacher demonstrated the experiment. (老师演示了实验。)
- This demonstrates the point. (这证明了这一点。)
- They demonstrated against the war. (他们示威反对战争。)

---
### 528. exemplify
**音标**: /ɪɡˈzemplɪfaɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 举例说明；作为…的典型
**英文释义**: to illustrate / to be a typical example
**词根**: Latin *exemplum*
**一词多义**:
- This story exemplifies kindness. (这个故事是善良的典型。)
- Let me exemplify the concept. (让我举例说明这个概念。)
- The painting exemplifies her style. (这幅画体现了她的风格。)

---
### 529. illustrate
**音标**: /ˈɪləstreɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 说明；给…加插图；表明
**英文释义**: to explain / to provide pictures / to show
**词根**: Latin *illustrare*
**一词多义**:
- The diagram illustrates the process. (图表说明了这个过程。)
- She illustrated the book. (她给书加了插图。)
- This case illustrates the problem. (这个案例表明了问题。)

---
### 530. depict
**音标**: /dɪˈpɪkt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 描绘；描写；描述
**英文释义**: to represent / to describe / to portray
**词根**: Latin *depingere*
**一词多义**:
- The painting depicts a scene. (这幅画描绘了一个场景。)
- The novel depicts rural life. (小说描写了乡村生活。)
- The photo depicts hardship. (照片描绘了艰辛。)

---
### 531. portray
**音标**: /pɔːˈtreɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 描绘；扮演；描写
**英文释义**: to represent / to act / to describe
**词根**: Old French *portraire*
**一词多义**:
- The movie portrays him as a hero. (电影把他描绘成英雄。)
- She portrays the character well. (她很好地扮演了这个角色。)
- The book portrays society. (这本书描写了社会。)

---
### 532. delineate
**音标**: /dɪˈlɪnɪeɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 描绘；描写；画轮廓
**英文释义**: to describe precisely / to outline
**词根**: Latin *delineare*
**一词多义**:
- The report delineates the process. (报告精确描述了这个过程。)
- She delineated her plans. (她描述了她的计划。)
- The map delineates the border. (地图画出了边界。)

---
### 533. embody
**音标**: /ɪmˈbɒdi/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 体现；包含；具体化
**英文释义**: to represent / to include / to personify
**词根**: Old English *in* + *bodig*
**一词多义**:
- She embodies kindness. (她体现了善良。)
- The book embodies our values. (这本书包含了我们的价值观。)
- The statue embodies the spirit. (雕像体现了精神。)

---
### 534. personify
**音标**: /pəˈsɒnɪfaɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 拟人化；是…的化身
**英文释义**: to give human qualities / to represent
**词根**: Latin *persona*
**一词多义**:
- The poem personifies the wind. (诗中将风拟人化。)
- She personifies elegance. (她是优雅的化身。)
- The character personifies evil. (这个角色象征着邪恶。)

---
### 535. epitomize
**音标**: /ɪˈpɪtəmaɪz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 成为…的典型；概括；代表
**英文释义**: to be a perfect example / to summarize
**词根**: Greek *epitome*
**一词多义**:
- She epitomizes success. (她是成功的典型。)
- This building epitomizes modern architecture. (这座建筑是现代建筑的典型代表。)
- The year epitomized change. (这一年象征着变革。)

---
### 536. symbolize
**音标**: /ˈsɪmbəlaɪz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 象征；代表；用符号表示
**英文释义**: to represent / to be a symbol of
**词根**: Greek *symbolon*
**一词多义**:
- The dove symbolizes peace. (鸽子象征和平。)
- The crown symbolizes royalty. (王冠象征着王权。)
- In math, x often symbolizes unknown. (在数学中x常代表未知数。)

---
### 537. signify
**音标**: /ˈsɪɡnɪfaɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 表示；意味着；象征
**英文释义**: to mean / to indicate / to represent
**词根**: Latin *signum*
**一词多义**:
- What does this sign signify? (这个符号表示什么？)
- Her nod signified approval. (她点头表示同意。)
- The word signifies both meanings. (这个词有两种含义。)

---
### 538. denote
**音标**: /dɪˈnəʊt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 表示；意味着；指示
**英文释义**: to indicate / to be a sign of / to mean
**词根**: Latin *denotare*
**一词多义**:
- Red often denotes danger. (红色常表示危险。)
- The symbol denotes a registered trademark. (该符号表示注册商标。)
- What does this term denote? (这个术语表示什么？)

---
### 539. imply
**音标**: /ɪmˈplaɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 暗示；意味；使必然
**英文释义**: to suggest / to hint / to entail
**词根**: Latin *implicare*
**一词多义**:
- What are you implying? (你暗示什么？)
- Silence implies consent. (沉默意味着同意。)
- The plan implies risk. (这个计划必然有风险。)

---
### 540. infer
**音标**: /ɪnˈfɜː/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 推断；推论；猜想
**英文释义**: to deduce / to conclude from evidence
**词根**: Latin *inferre*
**一词多义**:
- From the evidence, we infer guilt. (从证据推断出有罪。)
- What can we infer from this? (我们能从中推断出什么？)
- I infer that you're unhappy. (我猜想你不开心。)

---

## Day 19
### 541. deduce
**音标**: /dɪˈdjuːs/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 推断；演绎；推论
**英文释义**: to reach a conclusion from reasoning
**词根**: Latin *deducere*
**一词多义**:
- From the clues, we can deduce the answer. (从线索中我们可以推断出答案。)
- I deduce that she's not coming. (我推断她不来了。)
- What can you deduce from this? (你能从中推断出什么？)

---
### 542. derive
**音标**: /dɪˈraɪv/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 得自；起源；衍生
**英文释义**: to obtain / to originate / to trace
**词根**: Latin *derivare*
**一词多义**:
- We derive pleasure from reading. (我们从阅读中获得乐趣。)
- Many English words derive from Latin. (许多英语词源于拉丁语。)
- The theory derives from his research. (该理论源自他的研究。)

---
### 543. trace
**音标**: /treɪs/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 追踪；追溯；描摹；痕迹
**英文释义**: to follow / to find the origin / to copy / a mark
**词根**: Latin *tractus*
**一词多义**:
- We traced the source of the river. (我们追溯河流的源头。)
- The police traced the criminal. (警察追踪罪犯。)
- There are traces of ice on the road. (路上有冰的痕迹。)

---
### 544. attribute
**音标**: /əˈtrɪbjuːt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 归属；归因于
**英文释义**: to regard as caused by / to assign
**词根**: Latin *attribuere*
**一词多义**:
- We attribute success to hard work. (我们把成功归因于努力工作。)
- The painting is attributed to Leonardo. (这幅画被归属到达芬奇。)
- This song attributes fame to the singer. (这首歌把名誉归给歌手。)

---
### 545. ascribe
**音标**: /əˈskraɪb/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 归因于；归属于
**英文释义**: to assign / to attribute
**词根**: Greek *ascribein*
**一词多义**:
- He ascribes his failure to bad luck. (他把失败归因于运气不好。)
- The poem is ascribed to Shakespeare. (这首诗被归属到莎士比亚。)
- I ascribe no meaning to this. (我不对此作任何解释。)

---
### 546. correspond
**音标**: /ˌkɒrɪˈspɒnd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 相应；通信；一致
**英文释义**: to match / to communicate / to be alike
**词根**: Latin *correspondere*
**一词多义**:
- Her words correspond with her actions. (她的言行一致。)
- We correspond by email. (我们用邮件通信。)
- The two parts correspond to each other. (这两部分相互对应。)

---
### 547. conform
**音标**: /kənˈfɔːm/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 符合；遵守；适应
**英文释义**: to comply / to follow rules / to adapt
**词根**: Latin *conformare*
**一词多义**:
- We must conform to regulations. (我们必须遵守规定。)
- The building must conform to safety standards. (建筑必须符合安全标准。)
- She refuses to conform. (她拒绝随波逐流。)

---
### 548. comply
**音标**: /kəmˈplaɪ/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 遵从；顺从；答应
**英文释义**: to act in accordance / to obey
**词根**: Latin *complere*
**一词多义**:
- You must comply with the rules. (你必须遵守规则。)
- The company complies with regulations. (公司遵守法规。)
- She refused to comply. (她拒绝服从。)

---
### 549. adhere
**音标**: /ədˈhɪə/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 粘附；遵守；坚持
**英文释义**: to stick to / to follow / to hold firmly
**词根**: Latin *adhaerere*
**一词多义**:
- The stamp adheres to the envelope. (邮票粘在信封上。)
- We adhere to the principles. (我们遵守这些原则。)
- He adheres to his beliefs. (他坚持自己的信仰。)

---
### 550. coincide
**音标**: /ˌkəʊɪnˈsaɪd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 同时发生；一致；相符
**英文释义**: to occur at the same time / to agree / to match
**词根**: Latin *coincidere*
**一词多义**:
- Our vacation coincides with hers. (我们的假期和她的一致。)
- Their opinions coincide. (他们的意见一致。)
- The two events coincided. (两个事件同时发生。)

---
### 551. conformity
**音标**: /kənˈfɔːmɪti/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 一致；符合；遵奉
**英文释义**: compliance / agreement with rules
**词根**: Latin *conformis*
**一词多义**:
- In conformity with the rules. (遵守规则。)
- Conformity to social norms is expected. (遵守社会规范是被期望的。)
- His conformity impressed everyone. (他的一致性给大家留下了深刻印象。)

---
### 552. discrepancy
**音标**: /dɪsˈkrepənsi/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 差异；不符；矛盾
**英文释义**: a lack of agreement / a difference
**词根**: Latin *discrepantia*
**一词多义**:
- There's a discrepancy between the accounts. (两份陈述之间有差异。)
- The discrepancy in numbers is alarming. (数字差异令人担忧。)
- We noticed discrepancies in the data. (我们注意到数据中的差异。)

---
### 553. inconsistency
**音标**: /ˌɪnkənˈsɪstənsi/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 不一致；矛盾；反复无常
**英文释义**: lack of harmony / contradiction
**词根**: Latin *inconsistens*
**一词多义**:
- There's an inconsistency in his argument. (他的论点有不一致之处。)
- The report is full of inconsistencies. (报告充满矛盾。)
- Her inconsistency made her unreliable. (她的反复无常使她不可靠。)

---
### 554. coherence
**音标**: /kəʊˈhɪərəns/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 连贯性；一致性；凝聚
**英文释义**: logical connection / consistency
**词根**: Latin *cohaerere*
**一词多义**:
- The coherence of the argument is clear. (论证的连贯性很清楚。)
- We need coherence in our policies. (我们的政策需要一致性。)
- The story lacks coherence. (故事缺乏连贯性。)

---
### 555. compatibility
**音标**: /kəmˌpætɪˈbɪlɪti/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 兼容性；和谐共存
**英文释义**: ability to exist together / harmony
**词根**: Latin *compati*
**一词多义**:
- The compatibility of the systems is crucial. (系统的兼容性至关重要。)
- Check compatibility before buying. (购买前检查兼容性。)
- Their personalities have good compatibility. (他们的性格很合得来。)

---
### 556. correlation
**音标**: /ˌkɒrɪˈleɪʃən/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 相互关系；关联；相关
**英文释义**: a mutual relationship / connection
**词根**: Latin *correlatio*
**一词多义**:
- There's a correlation between smoking and cancer. (吸烟和癌症之间有关联。)
- The correlation is statistically significant. (这种相关性具有统计学意义。)
- We found a strong correlation. (我们发现了很强的相关性。)

---
### 557. correlation
**音标**: /ˌkɒrɪˈleɪʃən/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 关联；联系；相互关系
**英文释义**: mutual relation / connection
**词根**: Latin *correlatio*
**一词多义**:
- Study the correlation carefully. (仔细研究这种关联。)
- Correlation doesn't imply causation. (相关性不意味着因果关系。)

---
### 558. hypothesis
**音标**: /haɪˈpɒθɪsɪs/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 假设；假说
**英文释义**: a proposed explanation for observation
**词根**: Greek *hypothesis*
**一词多义**:
- Test this hypothesis. (检验这个假设。)
- The hypothesis needs revision. (这个假设需要修正。)
- Based on the hypothesis, we predict... (基于假设我们预测...)

---
### 559. premise
**音标**: /ˈpremɪs/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 前提；假定
**英文释义**: a statement accepted as true / basis
**词根**: Latin *praemittere*
**一词多义**:
- The premise of the argument is sound. (论证的前提是合理的。)
- Based on this premise... (基于这个前提...)
- The story's premise is interesting. (故事的前提很有趣。)

---
### 560. presumption
**音标**: /prɪˈzʌmpʃən/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 假定；推测；冒昧
**英文释义**: an assumption / arrogance
**词根**: Latin *praesumptio*
**一词多义**:
- The presumption that he was guilty. (假定他有罪。)
- I resent your presumptions. (我讨厌你的推测。)
- It would be presumption to assume. (假定是不礼貌的。)

---
### 561. postulate
**音标**: /ˈpɒstjʊleɪt/
**词性**: v./n.
**中文释义**: 假定；假设；公设
**英文释义**: to assume / a basic assumption
**词根**: Latin *postulare*
**一词多义**:
- We postulate that the theory is correct. (我们假定理论是正确的。)
- This is the main postulate of the theory. (这是该理论的主要假设。)
- His postulate seems reasonable. (他的假定看似合理。)

---
### 562. presuppose
**音标**: /ˌpriːsəˈpəʊz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 预先假设；以…为先决条件
**英文释义**: to assume in advance / to require
**词根**: Latin *praesupponere*
**一词多义**:
- This presupposes that you agree. (这以你同意为前提。)
- The theory presupposes certain conditions. (该理论以某些条件为前提。)
- Success presupposes preparation. (成功以准备为前提。)

---
### 563. theory
**音标**: /ˈθɪəri/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 理论；学说；意见
**英文释义**: a system of ideas / speculation
**词根**: Greek *theoria*
**一词多义**:
- In theory, this should work. (理论上这应该可行。)
- Evolution is a scientific theory. (进化论是科学理论。)
- She has a theory about it. (她对此有看法。)

---
### 564. theoretical
**音标**: /ˌθɪəˈretɪkəl/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 理论的；理论上的
**英文释义**: based on theory / not practical
**词根**: Greek *theoria*
**一词多义**:
- Theoretical physics is challenging. (理论物理学很有挑战性。)
- It's theoretically possible. (理论上是可能的。)
- The theoretical basis is solid. (理论基础是坚实的。)

---
### 565. framework

**英文释义**: a basic structure / a system

**一词多义**:
- The theoretical framework is established. (理论框架已经建立。)
- We need a framework for analysis. (我们需要分析框架。)
- The framework supports the building. (框架支撑着建筑。)

---
### 566. paradigm

**英文释义**: a typical model / a framework
**词根**: Greek *paradeigma*
**一词多义**:
- This represents a new paradigm. (这代表一个新范式。)
- A paradigm shift occurred. (发生了范式转变。)
- She is a paradigm of professionalism. (她是专业主义的典范。)

---
### 567. synthesis

**英文释义**: combining elements into a whole
**词根**: Greek *synthesis*
**一词多义**:
- The synthesis of these ideas. (这些思想的综合。)
- Protein synthesis occurs in cells. (蛋白质合成发生在细胞中。)
- This is a synthesis of various approaches. (这是各种方法的综合。)

---
### 568. analogy

**词性**: n.

**英文释义**: a comparison / similarity
**词根**: Greek *analogia*
**一词多义**:
- The analogy is helpful. (这个类比很有帮助。)
- By analogy, we can understand. (通过类比我们能够理解。)
- There's an analogy between them. (它们之间有相似之处。)

---
### 569. analogous
**音标**: /əˈnæləɡəs/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 类似的；相似的；可比的
**英文释义**: similar / comparable
**词根**: Greek *analogos*
**一词多义**:
- The brain is analogous to a computer. (大脑类似于电脑。)
- This situation is analogous to ours. (这种情况类似于我们的。)
- The two cases are analogous. (这两个案例是类似的。)

### 570. correlation

- The correlation coefficient is high. (相关系数很高。)

## Day 20
### 571. abstract
**音标**: /ˈæbstrækt/
**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 抽象的；摘要；提取
**英文释义**: theoretical / a summary / to extract
**词根**: Latin *abs* + *trahere*
**一词多义**:
- The concept is too abstract. (这个概念太抽象了。)
- Write an abstract of the article. (写文章的摘要。)
- Gold can be abstracted from ore. (金可以从矿石中提取。)

---
### 572. concrete
**音标**: /ˈkɒnkriːt/
**词性**: adj./n./v.
**中文释义**: 具体的；混凝土；使凝固
**英文释义**: specific / a building material / to solidify
**词根**: Latin *concretus*
**一词多义**:
- We need concrete evidence. (我们需要具体的证据。)
- The building is made of concrete. (建筑是混凝土做的。)
- Time will concrete the plan. (时间会使计划凝固。)

---
### 573. specific
**音标**: /spəˈsɪfɪk/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 具体的；特定的；明确的
**英文释义**: clearly defined / particular

**一词多义**:
- What are your specific needs? (你的具体需求是什么？)
- Each room has a specific purpose. (每个房间都有特定的用途。)
- Be more specific please. (请更明确一些。)

---
### 574. precise
**音标**: /prɪˈsaɪs/
**词性**: adj.

**英文释义**: exact / accurate / strict

- She is precise in her work. (她工作严谨。)

---
### 575. accurate
**音标**: /ˈækjʊrət/
**词性**: adj.

**英文释义**: correct / exact / truthful
**词根**: Latin *accurare*

- The data must be accurate. (数据必须准确。)
- Her description is accurate. (她的描述是准确的。)
- An accurate measurement. (准确的测量。)

---
### 576. exact
**音标**: /ɪɡˈzækt/
**词性**: adj./v.
**中文释义**: 精确的；准确的；强求
**英文释义**: precise / to demand / correct
**词根**: Latin *exigere*

- The exact amount is 100 dollars. (精确金额是100美元。)
- What are his exact words? (他的原话是什么？)
- I exact payment from you. (我要求你付款。)

---
### 577. definite
**音标**: /ˈdefɪnɪt/
**词性**: adj.

**英文释义**: clear / certain / limited
**词根**: Latin *definitus*

- I need a definite answer. (我需要一个明确的答复。)

---
### 578. explicit
**音标**: /ɪkˈsplɪsɪt/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 明确的；清楚的；详述的
**英文释义**: clearly expressed / detailed
**词根**: Latin *explicare*
**一词多义**:
- She was explicit about her intentions. (她明确表达了她的意图。)
- The instructions are explicit. (说明是清楚的。)

---
### 579. implicit

**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 含蓄的；不明确言明的；绝对的
**英文释义**: implied / understood / absolute
**词根**: Latin *implicare*
**一词多义**:
- It was implicit in her response. (她的回答含蓄地表达了这一点。)
- An implicit understanding. (默契。)
- She had implicit faith in him. (她对他绝对信任。)

---
### 580. vague
**音标**: /veɪɡ/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 模糊的；含糊的；不明确的
**英文释义**: not clear / not definite
**词根**: Latin *vagus*
**一词多义**:
- His answer was vague. (他的回答含糊不清。)
- I have only a vague idea. (我只有一个模糊的想法。)
- The shape is vague. (形状模糊。)

---
### 581. ambiguous
**音标**: /æmˈbɪɡjuəs/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 模糊不清的；引起歧义的
**英文释义**: unclear / having multiple meanings
**词根**: Latin *ambiguus*
**一词多义**:
- The wording is ambiguous. (措辞模糊不清。)
- The conclusion is ambiguous. (结论是模糊的。)
- Avoid ambiguous expressions. (避免含糊的表达。)

---
### 582. obscure
**音标**: /əbˈskjʊə/
**词性**: adj./v.
**中文释义**: 模糊的；昏暗的；遮蔽
**英文释义**: not clear / dark / to hide
**词根**: Latin *obscurus*
**一词多义**:
- The meaning is obscure. (意思模糊不清。)
- The obscure corner of the room. (房间昏暗的角落。)
- Clouds obscured the sun. (云遮住了太阳。)

---
### 583. nebulous

**英文释义**: cloud-like / vague / unclear
**词根**: Latin *nebula*
**一词多义**:
- His explanation was nebulous. (他的解释模糊不清。)
- Nebulous ideas about the future. (对未来的朦胧想法。)
- The concept is still nebulous. (概念仍然是模糊的。)

---
### 584. explicit
**音标**: /ɪkˈsplɪsɪt/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 明确的；清楚的；坦率的
**英文释义**: clearly stated / direct / frank
**词根**: Latin *explicare*
**一词多义**:
- The contract has explicit terms. (合同有明确的条款。)
- She was very explicit. (她非常坦率。)
- Explicit instructions were given. (给出了明确的指示。)

---
### 585. dichotomy
**音标**: /daɪˈkɒtəmi/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 二分法；分歧；对立
**英文释义**: division into two parts / contrast
**词根**: Greek *dichotomos*
**一词多义**:
- The false dichotomy in the argument. (论证中的错误二分法。)
- There's a dichotomy between theory and practice. (理论和实践之间存在分歧。)
- This reflects the dichotomy. (这反映了这种对立。)

---
### 586. polarize
**音标**: /ˈpəʊləraɪz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 使两极化；使对立
**英文释义**: to divide into opposite groups
**词根**: Greek *polos*
**一词多义**:
- The issue has polarized the public. (这个问题使公众两极分化。)
- Opinions are polarized. (观点两极分化。)
- This policy polarizes society. (这项政策使社会对立。)

---
### 587. binary
**音标**: /ˈbaɪnəri/
**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 二进制的；二元的；二元
**英文释义**: consisting of two parts / base-2
**词根**: Latin *binarius*
**一词多义**:
- Binary code is used in computers. (计算机使用二进制代码。)
- The binary system has only 0 and 1. (二进制系统只有0和1。)
- Binary opposition exists. (二元对立存在。)

---
### 588. duality
**音标**: /djuːˈælɪti/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 二元性；对立；双重性
**英文释义**: the state of having two parts
**词根**: Latin *duo*
**一词多义**:
- The duality of human nature. (人性的双重性。)
- There is a duality in his character. (他的性格有两面性。)
- This duality is fundamental. (这种二元性是根本的。)

---
### 589. juxtapose
**音标**: /ˈdʒʌkstəpəʊz/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 并置；把…并列
**英文释义**: to place side by side for comparison
**词根**: Latin *juxta* + *ponere*
**一词多义**:
- Juxtapose the two images. (把两张图片并置。)
- The artist juxtaposes modern and ancient. (艺术家把现代和古代并列。)
- This technique juxtaposes contrasts. (这种技术把对比并列。)

---
### 590. synthesis
**音标**: /ˈsɪnθɪsɪs/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 综合；合成
**英文释义**: combination into a coherent whole
**词根**: Greek *synthesis*
**一词多义**:
- A synthesis of the two theories. (两种理论的综合。)
- Chemical synthesis occurs. (发生化学合成。)
- The synthesis took months. (综合花了数月。)

---
### 591. integrate
**音标**: /ˈɪntɪɡreɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 使成整体；使结合；使一体化
**英文释义**: to combine / to join / to make whole
**词根**: Latin *integrare*
**一词多义**:
- We integrate theory with practice. (我们把理论和实践结合起来。)
- The new system integrates well. (新系统整合得很好。)
- Immigrants integrate into society. (移民融入社会。)

---
### 592. discrete
**音标**: /dɪsˈkriːt/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 离散的；不连续的；分离的
**英文释义**: separate / distinct / not continuous
**词根**: Latin *discretus*
**一词多义**:
- The data is discrete. (数据是离散的。)
- Three discrete parts. (三个分离的部分。)
- Discrete variables are used. (使用离散变量。)

---
### 593. fragment
**音标**: /ˈfræɡmənt/
**词性**: n./v.
**中文释义**: 碎片；片段；使破碎
**英文释义**: a piece / to break into pieces
**词根**: Latin *frangere*
**一词多义**:
- Only a fragment of the text remains. (只剩下文本的片段。)
- The glass fragmented. (玻璃碎了。)
- Don't fragment your attention. (不要分散你的注意力。)

---
### 594. coalesce
**音标**: /ˌkəʊəˈles/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 合并；结合；联合
**英文释义**: to come together / to merge
**词根**: Latin *coalescere*
**一词多义**:
- The two groups coalesced. (两组合并了。)
- Ideas coalesced into a plan. (想法合并成一个计划。)
- Bacteria coalesce to form colonies. (细菌结合成菌落。)

---
### 595. fusion
**音标**: /ˈfjuːʒən/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 融合；熔化；结合
**英文释义**: the process of merging / melting
**词根**: Latin *fundere*
**一词多义**:
- Nuclear fusion releases energy. (核聚变释放能量。)
- The fusion of cultures. (文化融合。)
- Their fusion created something new. (他们的结合创造了新的东西。)

---
### 596. amalgamate
**音标**: /əˈmælɡəmeɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 合并；混合；汞齐化
**英文释义**: to combine / to merge / to unite
**词根**: Latin *amalgama*
**一词多义**:
- The companies amalgamated. (公司合并了。)
- The alloy amalgamates metals. (合金混合金属。)
- Their views amalgamated over time. (他们的观点随时间融合了。)

---
### 597. hybrid
**音标**: /ˈhaɪbrɪd/
**词性**: n./adj.
**中文释义**: 混合体；杂种；混血儿
**英文释义**: a mixture / crossbred
**词根**: Greek *hybris*
**一词多义**:
- This is a hybrid of two species. (这是两个物种的杂交体。)
- Hybrid cars save fuel. (混合动力车省油。)
- The hybrid approach worked. (混合方法有效。)

---
### 598. synergy
**音标**: /ˈsɪnədʒi/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 协同作用；增效；协作
**英文释义**: combined effect greater than sum
**词根**: Greek *synergos*
**一词多义**:
- Team synergy improved productivity. (团队协作提高了生产力。)
- The synergy between the companies. (公司之间的协同作用。)
- Synergy created value. (协作创造了价值。)

---
### 599. synthesis
**音标**: /ˈsɪnθɪsɪs/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 综合；合成；综合体
**英文释义**: combining elements into a whole
**词根**: Greek *synthesis*
**一词多义**:
- The synthesis of findings. (发现的综合。)
- This requires careful synthesis. (这需要仔细综合。)
- Synthesis is key. (综合是关键。)

---
### 600. holistic
**音标**: /həˈlɪstɪk/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 整体的；全盘的
**英文释义**: considering the whole system
**词根**: Greek *holos*
**一词多义**:
- Take a holistic approach. (采取整体方法。)
- Holistic medicine treats the whole person. (整体医学治疗整个人。)
- We need a holistic view. (我们需要全局观。)

### 601. cognitive
**音标**: /ˈkɒɡnɪtɪv/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 认知的；认识的
**英文释义**: relating to thinking and understanding
**词根**: Latin *cognoscere*
**一词多义**:
- Cognitive development is important. (认知发展很重要。)
- Cognitive psychology studies mental processes. (认知心理学研究心理过程。)
- The cognitive approach is useful. (认知方法很有用。)

---
### 602. perception
**音标**: /pəˈsepʃən/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 感知；知觉；看法
**英文释义**: the ability to perceive / understanding
**词根**: Latin *percipere*
**一词多义**:
- Our perception of reality. (我们对现实的感知。)
- Public perception matters. (公众的看法很重要。)
- Sensory perception is limited. (感官知觉是有限的。)

---
### 603. consciousness
**音标**: /ˈkɒnʃəsnəs/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 意识；知觉；觉悟
**英文释义**: awareness / state of being awake
**词根**: Latin *conscire*
**一词多义**:
- Human consciousness is complex. (人类意识是复杂的。)
- She lost consciousness. (她失去了知觉。)
- Class consciousness exists. (阶级意识存在。)

---
### 604. subconscious
**音标**: /ˌsʌbˈkɒnʃəs/
**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 下意识的；潜意识
**英文释义**: below awareness / the unconscious mind
**词根**: Latin *sub* + *conscire*
**一词多义**:
- Subconscious thoughts influence behavior. (下意识的想法影响行为。)
- The subconscious mind is powerful. (潜意识很强大。)
- It happened on a subconscious level. (它发生在潜意识层面。)

---
### 605. intellect
**音标**: /ˈɪntɪlekt/
**词性**: n.
**中文释义**: 智力；理解力；知识分子
**英文释义**: the power of thinking / a clever person
**词根**: Latin *intellegere*
**一词多义**:
- Human intellect distinguishes us. (人类智力使我们独特。)
- He is a great intellect. (他是一位伟大的知识分子。)
- Intellect alone is not enough. (仅靠智力是不够的。)

### 606. rational
**音标**: /ˈræʃənəl/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 理性的；合理的
**英文释义**: based on reason / logical
**词根**: Latin *ratio*
**一词多义**:
- Humans are rational beings. (人类是理性生物。)
- A rational decision. (一个理性的决定。)
- Be rational about this. (对此要理性。)

---
### 607. irrational
**音标**: /ɪˈræʃənəl/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 不理性的；无理性的
**英文释义**: not based on reason / illogical
**词根**: Latin *ir* + *ratio*
**一词多义**:
- Irrational fear is common. (不理性的恐惧很常见。)
- The behavior seems irrational. (行为似乎不理性。)
- Irrational numbers exist in math. (数学中存在无理数。)

---
### 608. empirical
**音标**: /ɪmˈpɪrɪkəl/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 经验的；实证的
**英文释义**: based on observation or experiment
**词根**: Greek *empeirikos*
**一词多义**:
- Empirical evidence supports this. (实证证据支持这一点。)
- Empirical research is essential. (实证研究是必要的。)
- The empirical method is used. (使用实证方法。)

---
### 609. theoretical
**音标**: /ˌθɪəˈretɪkəl/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 理论的；理论上的
**英文释义**: based on theory / not practical
**词根**: Greek *theoria*
**一词多义**:
- Theoretical physics is abstract. (理论物理学是抽象的。)
- It's theoretically sound. (理论上站得住脚。)
- Theoretical frameworks guide research. (理论框架指导研究。)

---
### 610. abstract
**音标**: /ˈæbstrækt/
**词性**: adj./n./v.
**中文释义**: 抽象的；摘要；提取
**英文释义**: not concrete / summary / to extract
**词根**: Latin *abs* + *trahere*
**一词多义**:
- Abstract concepts are hard to grasp. (抽象概念难以理解。)
- Write an abstract for the paper. (为论文写摘要。)
- Extract and abstract the main points. (提取并摘要要点。)

---
### 611. concrete
**音标**: /ˈkɒnkriːt/
**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 具体的；混凝土
**英文释义**: specific / building material
**词根**: Latin *concretus*
**一词多义**:
- Concrete examples help understanding. (具体例子帮助理解。)
- We need concrete proposals. (我们需要具体的提议。)
- The wall is made of concrete. (墙是混凝土做的。)

---
### 612. subjective
**音标**: /səbˈdʒektɪv/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 主观的；主语的
**英文释义**: based on personal feelings / of the subject
**词根**: Latin *subjectus*
**一词多义**:
- Art is subjective. (艺术是主观的。)
- Subjective opinions vary. (主观意见各不相同。)
- The subjective case is nominative. (主格是主语格。)

---
### 613. objective
**音标**: /əbˈdʒektɪv/
**词性**: adj./n.
**中文释义**: 客观的；目标
**英文释义**: not biased / a goal
**词根**: Latin *obiectus*
**一词多义**:
- We need objective analysis. (我们需要客观分析。)
- The objective is clear. (目标是明确的。)
- Be objective about it. (对此要客观。)

---
### 614. neutral
**音标**: /ˈnjuːtrəl/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 中立的；中性的
**英文释义**: not supporting either side / chemically non-acidic
**词根**: Latin *neuter*
**一词多义**:
- Switzerland remains neutral. (瑞士保持中立。)
- The color is neutral. (颜色是中性的。)
- The solution is neutral. (溶液是中性的。)

---
### 615. bias
**音标**: /ˈbaɪəs/
**词性**: n./v.
**中文释义**: 偏见；倾向；使有偏见
**英文释义**: prejudice / to influence unfairly
**词根**: Greek *biasis*
**一词多义**:
- The judge showed no bias. (法官没有表现出偏见。)
- Cultural bias exists. (文化偏见存在。)
- The study was biased. (研究有偏见。)

---
### 616. prejudice
**音标**: /ˈpredʒʊdɪs/
**词性**: n./v.
**中文释义**: 偏见；成见；损害
**英文释义**: preconceived opinion / to harm
**词根**: Latin *prae* + *judicium*
**一词多义**:
- Racial prejudice is wrong. (种族偏见是错误的。)
- Without prejudice to your claim. (不损害你的索赔权。)
- Don't prejudice the outcome. (不要偏见结果。)

---
### 617. discriminate
**音标**: /dɪˈskrɪmɪneɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 歧视；区别；辨别
**英文释义**: to treat unfairly / to distinguish
**词根**: Latin *discriminare*
**一词多义**:
- The law prohibits discrimination. (法律禁止歧视。)
- You must discriminate between fact and opinion. (你必须区分事实和观点。)
- She can discriminate colors well. (她辨别颜色的能力很强。)

---
### 618. objective
**音标**: /əbˈdʒektɪv/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 客观的；真实的
**英文释义**: based on facts / not personal
**词根**: Latin *obiectus*
**一词多义**:
- An objective observer. (客观的观察者。)
- Try to be objective. (尽量客观。)
- The objective truth. (客观真理。)

---
### 619. partial
**音标**: /ˈpɑːʃəl/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 部分的；偏袒的；偏爱的
**英文释义**: incomplete / biased / having a liking
**词根**: Latin *pars*
**一词多义**:
- The solution is only partial. (解决方案只是部分的。)
- The referee was partial. (裁判偏袒。)
- I'm partial to chocolate. (我偏爱巧克力。)

---
### 620. impartial
**音标**: /ɪmˈpɑːʃəl/
**词性**: adj.

**英文释义**: fair / unbiased
**词根**: Latin *in* + *pars*
**一词多义**:
- An impartial jury. (公正的陪审团。)
- We need impartial advice. (我们需要公正的建议。)
- Be impartial in your judgment. (判断要公正。)

---
### 621. biased
**音标**: /ˈbaɪəst/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 有偏见的；偏向的
**英文释义**: showing prejudice / one-sided
**词根**: Greek *biasis*
**一词多义**:
- The media is often biased. (媒体常常有偏见。)
- A biased source. (有偏见的来源。)
- He is biased against foreigners. (他对外人有偏见。)

---
### 622. prejudiced
**音标**: /ˈpredʒʊdɪst/
**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 有偏见的；怀有成见的
**英文释义**: having preconceived opinions
**词根**: Latin *prae* + *judicium*
**一词多义**:
- A prejudiced view. (有偏见的观点。)
- Don't be prejudiced. (不要有成见。)
- He is prejudiced against women. (他歧视女性。)

### 623. rationale

**英文释义**: the underlying reason / basis

### 624. justification
**音标**: /ˌdʒʌstɪfɪˈkeɪʃən/
**词性**: n.

**英文释义**: a reason for something / defense

- What is the justification? (理由是什么？)

### 625. criterion

### 626. standard

### 627. norm

- Social norms exist. (社会规范存在。)
- The norm in this culture. (这种文化的规范。)

### 628. parameter
**音标**: /pəˈræmɪtə/

**英文释义**: a measurable factor / a limit

- Within acceptable parameters. (在可接受的范围内。)

---
### 629. benchmark
**音标**: /ˈbentʃmɑːk/
**词性**: n./v.

**英文释义**: a standard for comparison / to measure against

- This is the benchmark. (这是基准。)
- Benchmark your performance. (将你的表现作为基准。)
- Industry benchmarks are set. (设定了行业基准。)

### 630. baseline

**英文释义**: a starting point / a reference line
**词根**: Old English *base* + *line*
**一词多义**:

- Compare against the baseline. (与基线比较。)
- The baseline data is essential. (基线数据是必不可少的。)

### 632. perspicacious

**英文释义**: having keen insight / shrewd
**词根**: Latin *perspicax*
**一词多义**:
- A perspicacious analysis. (敏锐的分析。)
- She is perspicacious about human nature. (她对人性有敏锐的洞察力。)

### 633. sagacious
**音标**: /səˈɡeɪʃəs/
**词性**: adj.

### 661. obfuscate
**音标**: /ˈɒbfʌskeɪt/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 使模糊；使困惑；使难懂
**英文释义**: to confuse / to make unclear
**词根**: Latin *obfuscare*
**一词多义**:
- Don't obfuscate the issue. (不要模糊问题。)
- The speaker obfuscated the truth. (演讲者模糊了真相。)
- Attempts to obfuscate failed. (模糊问题的尝试失败了。)

---
### 662. confound
**音标**: /kənˈfaʊnd/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 使困惑；使混淆；使惊讶
**英文释义**: to perplex / to mix up / to surprise
**词根**: Latin *confundere*
**一词多义**:
- The results confounded expectations. (结果出乎意料。)
- Don't confound the two issues. (不要混淆两个问题。)
- The mystery confounded investigators. (谜团让调查者困惑。)

---
### 663. perplex
**音标**: /pəˈpleks/
**词性**: v.
**中文释义**: 使困惑；使复杂化
**英文释义**: to puzzle / to complicate
**词根**: Latin *perplexus*
**一词多义**:
- The problem perplexed everyone. (问题让每个人困惑。)
- Don't perplex the matter further. (不要再使事情复杂化。)
- We were perplexed by the decision. (我们对这个决定感到困惑。)

---
### 664. bewilder

**英文释义**: to confuse / to puzzle greatly

- The复杂 instructions bewildered her. (复杂的说明使她困惑。)
- The sudden change bewildered everyone. (突然的变化使每个人不知所措。)
- bewildered by the question. (被问题弄糊涂了。)

---
### 665. stupefy

- The beauty stupefied viewers. (美丽使观众惊叹。)

### 670. exasperate

### 673. provoke

### 675. enrage

### 676. exasperate

**英文释义**: lasting a short time / fleeting
**词根**: Greek *ephemeros*
**一词多义**:
- Fame can be ephemeral. (名声可以是短暂的。)
- The ephemeral beauty of cherry blossoms. (樱花的短暂美丽。)
- Our worries seem ephemeral. (我们的担忧似乎是短暂的。)

### 722. transient

### 723. fleeting

---
### 724. evanescent

### 781. anachronism

- The anachronism in the film. (电影中的时代错误。)

**词性**: adj.
**中文释义**: 至上的；首要的；最高的
**英文释义**: supreme / of the highest importance
**词根**: Latin *par* + *mount*
**一词多义**:
- Paramount importance. (至上的重要性。)
- The paramount chief. (最高首领。)
- This is of paramount concern. (这是首要的关注。)

---

**英文释义**: first / main / basic
**词根**: Latin *primus*

- Primary education. (初级教育。)
- The primary color. (原色。)

### 818. principal

### 819. prime

**英文释义**: first / best / the best period / to prepare

- The prime of life. (人生的黄金时期。)
